Mobile safety was verified by layout measurement at a 390px container width (overflow 0). The browser console reported no errors or warnings.

Onboarding Success admission decisions:

- Visual surface: admitted. The confirmation card, icon tiles, display-ID cards, "what's next" section, and actions are converted to canonical calm tokens and pass strict radius; `OnboardingSuccessPage.jsx` is in the default hardgate.
- Local semantic color: admitted calm. The success/missing icons, display-ID markers, and "what's next" icons use muted non-danger tones, and the primary actions are neutral. Brand red is removed from the confirmation surface; red stays reserved for real danger.
- Success-state source: excluded until receiver proof. The route still has no canonical read owner; a direct visit or refresh loses `location.state.result` and shows the calm degraded missing-state instead of success copy without organization/user evidence.
- Display IDs: excluded until receiver proof. The route labels `Organization ID` from `organization.display_id`, but Page 21 still blocks organization-vs-hospital identity because current onboarding inserts into `hospitals` and stores that id in `profiles.organization_id`.
- Review timing and dashboard-access: excluded until receiver proof. The active copy is honest (`Review follows the admin queue`, `Open console`); it makes no `24-48 hours` SLA or dashboard-eligibility promise, and those stay unproved until a verification/queue/route-access receiver exists.
- Support handoff: preserved as a simple fallback. The `mailto:` link is not admitted as a monitored support receiver.
- Direct-link/refresh behavior: proven degraded state. Missing `location.state` shows the calm degraded missing-state and return-to-registration action; a canonical refetch by registration/organization/facility/user id is still not proved.
- Shell/layout: proven public-shell exception. Rendered proof confirmed no authenticated nav, bottom bar, or startup overlay leaks onto `/onboarding-success`; it does not define a second global shell pattern for console pages.
- Hardgate: `OnboardingSuccessPage.jsx` is now in the default `scripts/check-ui-surface-hardgate.js` set and passes strict radius.

Required before Page 22 can move past intake:

1. Define one success-state owner for submitted registration evidence, organization/facility identity, admin identity, verification status, dashboard eligibility, direct-link recovery, support handoff, loading states, and degraded states.
2. Prove whether the page is allowed to rely on navigation state only or must refetch by a canonical registration/organization/user id.
3. Prove the organization/facility identity model carried from Page 21 before rendering `Organization Identity` as product truth.
4. Prove the verification queue, review timing, notification path, and admin receiver before preserving or rewriting review-time copy.
5. Prove dashboard route access for completed, pending, skipped, failed-profile-update, and missing-profile states after submit.
6. Prove the support receiver or keep the link as a simple fallback that does not promise monitored case handling.
7. Prove `/login`, `/set-password`, `/onboarding`, and `/onboarding-success` redirect/deep-link behavior together before changing public auth/registration copy or flow order.
8. Add desktop, tablet, and mobile rendered proof with no authenticated-shell leakage, no startup overlay, no duplicate nav, visible missing-state behavior, and immediate feedback on dashboard/support links.
9. Add hardgate coverage only after the public confirmation visual system is converted to shared tokens, squircle geometry, structural loading/error states, receiver-backed copy, and intentional motion.

### Page 23 Admission - Unauthorized

Audit status: intake started 2026-07-06; the Unauthorized visual surface (the `UnauthorizedPage` export inside `ProtectedRoute.jsx`) is admitted for guarded continuation on 2026-07-07 after its first visual pass and desktop/mobile-safety/interaction rendered proof. Decision: `UnauthorizedPage` is admitted under the Today/Requests canon as a public denied-state recovery surface only. It is not a reusable global pattern source, it does not reuse Requests multi-data language, and it does not resolve the coordinated public-route redirect/flow-order or single denied-state-owner decisions. `ProtectedRoute.jsx` was already in the default UI hardgate as shared guard code; the Unauthorized visual surface within it now passes strict radius and speaks the calm canonical token system.

Why Unauthorized is Page 23:

- `/unauthorized` is a public route mounted outside `ProtectedRoute`.
- `routes.jsx` names `/unauthorized` as `public: true`, and `ProtectedRoute.jsx` includes it in the public allowed path list.
- `AppShell` hides the authenticated console shell, shared nav, right context panel, FAB, bottom bar, and startup overlay for `/unauthorized`; this is a public denied-state exception, not a reusable console page layout.
- `ProtectedRoute.jsx` redirects to `/unauthorized` from several authority checks: missing profile with `authError`, inaccessible navigation path, role mismatch, minimum-role mismatch, and resource permission failure.
- The route crosses multiple authority owners: AuthProvider profile load state, `authError.type`, navigation access, role hierarchy, `can('view', resource)`, redirect origin state, visible role/email rendering, back navigation, Today navigation, and sign-out.

HEAD snapshot evidence for this ledger: `git show HEAD:frontend/src/components/common/ProtectedRoute.jsx`, `git show HEAD:frontend/src/App.js`, `git show HEAD:frontend/src/config/routes.jsx`, `git show HEAD:frontend/src/contexts/AuthContext.jsx`, and `git show HEAD:frontend/src/config/navigation.js` showed the old behavior inventory: unauthenticated protected routes redirect to `/login`, missing profile stayed on `DynamicAuthSkeleton`, inaccessible nav/role/resource checks redirect to `/unauthorized`, public allowed paths include `/unauthorized`, `UnauthorizedPage` used old `Access Restricted` copy, `clearance level` copy, decorative background orbs, emoji stop icon, `Current Identity`, `Go Back`, `Return to Dashboard`, sign-out icon button, and no explicit missing-profile reason copy.

- Targeted recent history check: `git log --since='2026-06-28' -- frontend/src/components/common/ProtectedRoute.jsx frontend/src/App.js frontend/src/config/routes.jsx frontend/src/contexts/AuthContext.jsx frontend/src/config/navigation.js` returned no newer committed changes for those anchor files. The Unauthorized preservation baseline for this intake pass remains committed `HEAD` at `f31f29f`; active Unauthorized work is uncommitted source on top of that baseline.
- Active source recertification: `ProtectedRoute.jsx` now routes missing-profile `authError` to `/unauthorized` with `state.reason`, classifies `profile_missing`, `profile_unavailable`, and `session_unavailable` as `Account not ready`, uses `You do not have access` for ordinary denial, renders `Your console profile is not ready yet.` or `Your current role (...) cannot open this page.`, uses the lucide `LockKeyhole` icon instead of the old emoji stop icon, removes old decorative background orbs/top bar, labels the profile badge `Current role`, provides `Go back`, `Go to Today`, and sign-out actions, and keeps `/unauthorized` shell-hidden.

- First Unauthorized visual pass on 2026-07-07 admitted the `UnauthorizedPage` denied-state surface. It converted the old `squircle-2xl`/`squircle-xl`/`squircle-lg`/`squircle-sm` radii and the sign-out `rounded-full` into canonical `rounded-card`, `rounded-icon`, `rounded-inner`, `rounded-button`, and `rounded-pill` tokens; removed `shadow-2xl`, `shadow-glow`, `shadow-inner`, and the `backdrop-blur-xs` decorative blur; removed `tracking-tighter`/`tracking-widest`/`tracking-wide` chrome; moved the lock tile and icon off `bg-destructive/10`/`text-destructive` to muted non-danger `bg-muted`/`text-muted-foreground` (access denial is not danger); moved the `Go to Today` primary off brand red to a neutral theme-safe `bg-foreground`/`text-background`; and neutralized the admin role chip off `bg-primary/20` to `bg-foreground/10`. It preserved the denial logic, `reason`/`missingProfile` classification, `Account not ready` / `You do not have access` copy, the `Current role` badge, and the `Go back`, `Go to Today`, and sign-out receivers with their pending feedback.

The `squircle-*` radii, `rounded-full`, decorative shadows/blur, tracking chrome, and destructive lock/primary color are converted this pass. The denial logic, `authError` reason routing, `signOut()` receiver, and `Go back`/`Go to Today` navigation are preserved. The single denied-state owner, redirect-origin detail, per-denial recovery handoffs, and coordinated public-route flow order remain excluded until receiver proof, as separate cross-route decisions rather than this visual pass.

Mobile safety was verified by layout measurement at a 390px container width (the card constrains to 350px; `Go back`, `Go to Today`, and sign-out fit at 286/230/48px; overflow 0); a true 390px viewport screenshot was blocked by the local window minimum width. Unauthorized admission decisions:

- Visual surface: admitted. The `UnauthorizedPage` denied-state card, lock tile, denial copy, role badge, and actions are converted to canonical calm tokens and pass strict radius as part of `ProtectedRoute.jsx`.
- Local semantic color: admitted calm. The lock and title use muted non-danger tones, the primary action is neutral, and the role badge is neutral. Red is no longer used for the denied state; it stays reserved for real danger.

- Sign-out receiver: preserved. The click still awaits `signOut()` before navigating to `/login` and shows toast feedback on failure; broader Auth receiver outcome and session cleanup proof remain outside this visual pass.
- Redirect origin and denied-state owner: excluded until receiver proof. Ordinary role/nav/resource redirects still do not preserve which route, resource, min role, or capability failed, and there is not yet one denied-state owner distinguishing missing profile, unavailable profile, role denial, and resource denial. These are cross-route/authority decisions, not this visual pass.

- Shell/layout: proven public-shell exception. Rendered proof confirmed no authenticated nav, bottom bar, or startup overlay leaks onto `/unauthorized`; it does not define a second global shell pattern for console pages.
- Hardgate: `ProtectedRoute.jsx` remains in the default `scripts/check-ui-surface-hardgate.js` set as shared guard code, and the Unauthorized visual surface inside it now passes strict radius.

Cross-route flow work still open (separate from this denied-state visual admission):

1. Define one denied-state owner for unauthenticated, missing profile, unavailable profile, onboarding-pending, route-not-in-nav, role mismatch, min-role mismatch, resource denial, and unknown denial states.

3. Prove AuthProvider `authError.type` values, missing-profile recovery behavior, session-unavailable behavior, and support handoff.
4. Prove role/resource access semantics for provider, sponsor, viewer, org admin, and admin denials without leaking protected data.
5. Prove `Go back`, `Go to Today`, and sign-out receiver behavior with immediate pressed/pending/redirect/failure feedback.
6. Prove `/login`, `/set-password`, `/onboarding`, `/onboarding-success`, and `/unauthorized` redirect/deep-link behavior together before changing public auth/denied-state copy or flow order.
7. Add desktop, tablet, and mobile rendered proof with no authenticated-shell leakage, no startup overlay, no duplicate nav, visible missing-profile and role-denied states, and no stale control-room copy.
8. Keep `ProtectedRoute.jsx` hardgate-clean as shared guard code, then mark Unauthorized visual admission only after rendered denied-state proof closes this ledger.

### Page 24 Admission - Catch-All Not Found

Audit status: intake started 2026-07-06; admitted for guarded route-recovery continuation on 2026-07-07 after the first visual pass and desktop/light-theme/interaction rendered proof plus a 390px layout-safety measurement. Decision: the `*` catch-all `NotFoundPage` is admitted under the Today/Requests canon as a public route-recovery surface only. It is not a reusable global pattern source, it does not reuse Requests multi-data language, and it does not decide unknown-route redirect/flow-order behavior: rendering a calm 404 stays the chosen behavior for unknown URLs, while deprecated-route alias mapping remains a separate cross-public-route flow decision.

Why Catch-All Not Found is Page 24:

- `App.js` mounts `<Route path="*" element={<NotFoundPage />} />`.
- `NotFoundPage.jsx` is a lazy route import and is not wrapped in `ProtectedRoute`.
- `AppShell` now hides chrome for unknown paths through `shouldHideShellChrome(pathname)`, which treats the known public shell paths separately from the authenticated console route list. This is a first shell-chrome cleanup only; AuthProvider, PageDataProvider, MapProvider, and route recovery behavior still need proof before the catch-all is admitted.
- `NotFoundPage.jsx` previously called `usePageHeader("Lost Signal", null)`, which made an unknown route participate in shared header state even though route ownership, auth state, and public/private visibility were not proved.
- The route crosses multiple authority owners: router fallback behavior, AuthProvider state, app-shell visibility, unknown deep-link recovery, browser history, Today redirect, and stale/deprecated route handling.

HEAD snapshot evidence for this ledger: `git show HEAD:frontend/src/App.js`, `git show HEAD:frontend/src/components/pages/NotFoundPage.jsx`, `git show HEAD:frontend/src/config/routes.jsx`, and `git show HEAD:frontend/src/components/common/ProtectedRoute.jsx` showed the old/current behavior inventory: lazy `NotFoundPage` import, catch-all route outside `ProtectedRoute`, `usePageHeader("Lost Signal", null)`, `Card` wrapper, decorative destructive gradient and blurred orb, `404`, `Signal Lost`, haiku copy, `Go Back`, `Return to Dashboard`, `navigate(-1)`, `navigate('/')`, and no route protection or shell-hidden exception for unknown paths.

- Targeted recent history check: `git log --since='2026-06-28' -- frontend/src/components/pages/NotFoundPage.jsx frontend/src/App.js frontend/src/config/routes.jsx frontend/src/components/common/ProtectedRoute.jsx` returned no newer committed changes for those anchor files. The Catch-All preservation baseline for this intake pass remains committed `HEAD` at `f31f29f`; active Not Found source is unchanged on top of that baseline.
- Active source recertification: `App.js` still imports `NotFoundPage` lazily and mounts it for `path="*"`. `App.js` now centralizes shell visibility with `PUBLIC_SHELL_ROUTES`, `AUTHENTICATED_SHELL_ROUTES`, `normalizeShellPath()`, and `shouldHideShellChrome()`, so unknown paths no longer inherit the authenticated shell chrome by default. As of the intake pass, `NotFoundPage.jsx` still rendered `404`, `Signal Lost`, haiku copy, `Go Back`, and `Return to Dashboard`, imported `Card`, and navigated with browser history or `/`; the 2026-07-07 visual pass below converts that surface. It no longer writes shared page-header state through `usePageHeader`. `routes.jsx` has no explicit catch-all route protection entry, and `ProtectedRoute.jsx` does not govern the catch-all route.
- First catch-all shell/action cleanup on 2026-07-06 removed `usePageHeader("Lost Signal", null)` from the active catch-all page so unknown routes no longer write shared header state while shell ownership is unresolved. The same cleanup added local pending feedback and duplicate-tap blocking for `Go Back` and `Return to Dashboard` without admitting the route.
- First Catch-All visual pass on 2026-07-07 admitted `NotFoundPage.jsx` as a public route-recovery surface. It converted the old decorative destructive gradient, blurred orb (`bg-destructive/10 rounded-full blur-[80px]`), `squircle-3xl`/`squircle-2xl`/`squircle-xl` radii, `shadow-2xl`/`shadow-glow`, the `animate-pulse-slow` looping animation, `tracking-tighter` chrome, the `Card` wrapper, the `Signal Lost` title, the haiku copy, and the `Return to Dashboard` stale label into a calm canonical surface: a `rounded-card` panel, a `rounded-icon` compass tile, muted non-danger color, plain `404` / `Page not found` copy, one neutral primary `Go to Today` action (`bg-foreground`/`text-background`, theme-safe), and a `Go back` secondary. It preserved the `navigate(-1)` / `navigate('/')` recovery targets and the `pendingAction` immediate-feedback contract (`Opening previous page...` / `Opening Today...`, disabled duplicate taps). `NotFoundPage.jsx` is now in the default UI hardgate and passes `npm run check:ui-hardgate:revamp -- src/components/pages/NotFoundPage.jsx`.
- Catch-All preservation ledger: the old `usePageHeader("Lost Signal")` shared-header write is converted (removed; unknown routes no longer write header state). The old decorative orb/gradient/glow/pulse chrome and `squircle-*` radii are converted to canonical calm tokens. The old `Signal Lost` / haiku / `Return to Dashboard` copy is converted to plain route-recovery language. The `navigate(-1)` and `navigate('/')` recovery targets and the pending/duplicate-tap feedback are preserved. Unknown-route redirect and deprecated-alias mapping plus coordinated public-route flow order remain excluded until receiver proof, as a separate cross-public-route decision rather than this page's visual pass.
- Rendered proof, 2026-07-07: reused the running `localhost:3000` dev server (no new server spawned) and drove the local Chrome. `/not-a-real-page?proof=notfound-desktop-neutral-2026-07-07` and `/still-not-real` rendered the revamped 404 with the neutral `Go to Today` primary, no authenticated nav/FAB/bottom-bar/context-panel (`shouldHideShellChrome`), no framework overlay, and zero horizontal overflow (`scrollWidth - innerWidth = 0`). The page rendered correctly in both dark and light theme, and the `bg-foreground`/`text-background` primary stays high-contrast in each; the app's initial-paint theme transition is a pre-existing global `ThemeProvider` behavior, not a Page 24 concern. Mobile safety was verified by layout measurement at a 390px container width (the card constrains to 350px, both actions are full-width at 286px, horizontal overflow 0); a true 390px viewport screenshot was not captured because the local browser window could not shrink below ~1500px in this environment. Interaction proof: clicking `Go to Today` rendered the disabled `Opening Today...` pending state and navigated to `/`. The browser console reported no errors or warnings for the route.

Catch-All admission decisions:

- Route ownership: render-404 admitted for unknown URLs. The `*` route renders the public `NotFoundPage` with shell chrome hidden and offers safe recovery. It still has no explicit `routes.jsx` config entry, and deprecated-route alias mapping remains a separate cross-public-route flow decision.
- Auth/shell exposure: proven for this page. Unknown paths no longer inherit app chrome, context panel, FAB, bottom bar, footer, or startup overlay, and rendered proof confirmed no authenticated nav/FAB/bottom-bar/context-panel leakage. The route still runs inside the app provider tree; the signed-out unknown route triggers no protected domain read.
- Missing-route recovery: generic recovery admitted. The page offers `Go to Today` and `Go back` with immediate feedback. It does not yet distinguish typo, stale link, deprecated route, unauthorized route, or signed-out state, which stays future copy/flow work rather than a blocker for this calm generic surface.
- Copy: converted and admitted. `Lost Signal`, `Signal Lost`, `void space`, the haiku, and `Return to Dashboard` are replaced with plain `Page not found` route-recovery copy plus `Go to Today` / `Go back`.
- Action feedback: proven. `Go to Today` and `Go back` show pending copy and disable duplicate taps; rendered proof captured the `Opening Today...` disabled pending state and navigation to `/`.
- Shell/layout: resolved for this page. The catch-all renders a public shell-hidden 404 through `shouldHideShellChrome()`; rendered proof confirmed no authenticated shell leakage and zero horizontal overflow.
- Hardgate: `NotFoundPage.jsx` is now in the default `scripts/check-ui-surface-hardgate.js` set and passes strict-radius; the decorative orb and legacy `squircle-*` radii are gone.

Cross-route flow work still open (separate from this page-recovery admission):

1. Define an explicit `routes.jsx` owner classification for unknown paths, stale links, and deprecated route names.
2. Decide any deprecated-route alias mapping and whether specific old paths should redirect instead of rendering the 404.
3. Prove `/login`, `/set-password`, `/onboarding`, `/onboarding-success`, `/unauthorized`, and `*` redirect/deep-link behavior together before changing coordinated public auth/route-recovery flow order.
4. Extend recovery copy to distinguish typo, stale link, deprecated route, signed-out, and role-denied users if a future product decision requires it.

Catch-All Requests-canon blocker map:

This is a blocker map, not a design target. Page 24 may not reuse Requests visual language because the catch-all is a route-recovery handoff, not a multi-data work stage.

| Requests canon slot | Catch-All current evidence | Page 24 admission decision |
|---|---|---|
| Signal field | The page opens as `404` / `Page not found` with one calm line. | Admitted. Route-recovery signal says the page was not found and what to do next. |
| State choices | There are no operational filters or status choices. | Do not copy Requests state cards. Preserve one safe primary recovery action. |
| Handled sheet | There is no operational handled sheet. | Not applicable. Catch-all should stay focused on recovery and safe routing. |
| Focused detail | The current detail is decorative copy only. | Intake only. Must prove route source and recovery behavior before visual promotion. |
| Route-owned action | `Go to Today` (neutral primary) and `Go back` (secondary) with pending copy and duplicate-tap blocking. | Admitted. Rendered proof captured the `Opening Today...` pending state and navigation; no-history and auth-dependent outcomes stay future flow work. |
| Shared modal/sheet | Catch-all uses no console `ModalShell`/`FilterSheet`. | Route-recovery exception. Do not introduce private modal patterns from this page into authenticated pages. |
| Mobile recomposition | The centered card is responsive; a 390px layout measurement showed 0 horizontal overflow with the card at 350px and full-width actions. | Admitted for mobile safety by measurement. A true 390px viewport screenshot was blocked by the local window minimum width. |
| Data quieting | AuthProvider and app providers wrap the route; `AppShell` now hides chrome for unknown paths through `shouldHideShellChrome()`. | Safer, not admitted. Future pass must prove or repair provider behavior and route ownership on unknown paths. |
| Local semantic color | Current UI uses muted non-danger color; the old destructive red and decorative gradients are removed (a missing route is not danger). | Admitted. Red stays reserved for real danger; route-recovery uses calm neutral/muted tones. |
| Interaction feedback | Entry animation, pending navigation copy, and disabled duplicate taps exist. | Intake only. Future proof must cover no-history back behavior, Today redirect, and auth/profile redirects. |

Promotion note: the first Catch-All visual pass is complete. `NotFoundPage.jsx` is converted to canonical calm tokens, admitted as a public route-recovery surface, and added to the default hardgate. Page 24 remains a route-recovery exception (not a Requests multi-data pattern source), and unknown-route redirect/deprecated-alias flow order stays a separate cross-public-route decision.

### Page 10 Admission - Support

Audit status: started on 2026-07-03 and admitted for guarded continuation after source, build, encoding, and rendered Browser/IAB proof. Decision: Support at `/support-tickets` is admitted under the Today/Requests canon for this page only. It is not promoted as a reusable global pattern source.

Route and RBAC:

- `App.js` mounts `/support-tickets` behind `ProtectedRoute minRole="provider"`.
- `routes.jsx` names `/support-tickets` as `minRole: 'provider'`, `resource: 'support'`, title `Support`, and `excludedRoles: ['sponsor']`.
- `navigation.js` exposes Support under management/help for provider and above, with sponsors excluded.
- `consoleModuleRail.js` maps the compact rail item `Help` to `/support-tickets`.
- `ProtectedRoute.jsx` checks the accessible navigation before the `minRole`, so the sponsor exclusion depends on the route staying absent from sponsor nav.

HEAD snapshot evidence for this ledger: `git show HEAD:frontend/src/components/pages/SupportTicketsPage.jsx` and `git show HEAD:frontend/src/services/supportTicketsService.js` showed old `useSupportTickets` orchestration, direct `support_tickets` service reads/writes, `ViewToggle`, grid/list/table modes, `FilterSheet`, `AnalyticsModal`, `SupportTicketModal`, `ConfirmationModal`, `BulkActionBar`, single delete, bulk delete, assignment, status updates, footer pagination, custom `openSupportTicketModal` and `openFilters` window events, mobile pull-to-refresh, load-more, local KPI/search filtering, fake chart data, local analytics counts, and private modal chrome.

Current source owner and conversion state:

- `SupportTicketsPage.jsx` owns the visible route through the shared shell: `usePageHeader()`, `usePageFooter(null, 'status', false)`, and `usePageShell({ bleed: true, hideFab: true })`. It renders one support signal, service-backed state choices, a handled queue sheet, a focused desktop detail rail, and shared modal/reveal surfaces.
- `supportTicketsService.js` now exposes `getSupportTicketsPage()` as the route projection owner with scoped auth filtering, exact count, scoped status stats, server pagination, sorting, search, and normalized rows. The page treats failed support truth as an error state instead of a clear queue.
- `pageDataAccess.js` leaves `/support-tickets` route-owned, so `PageDataContext` does not duplicate support truth on the Support route.
- `SupportTicketsPage.jsx` now publishes `supportPanelContext` from the same route projection that renders the page. `ContextPanel.jsx` requests `requestSupportTicketsRouteContext` and renders `<SupportTicketsPanel supportContext={supportTicketsRouteContext} />`, so the right panel no longer consumes broad `PageDataContext` support data or performs its own direct `support_tickets` read/subscription.
- `ContextAwareFAB.jsx` and `DynamicBottomBar.jsx` now treat `/support-tickets` as route-owned. Desktop does not mount the global support FAB path on the route, and mobile owns the route action through `New ticket`.
- `SupportTicketModal.jsx` now uses shared `ModalShell`. The active create/edit path submits only `subject`, `message`, `category`, and `priority`; generic create/update no longer accepts `assigned_to` or `status` from form payloads.
- Single delete, bulk delete, and provider self-assign are restored on the active Support surface (2026-07-08 re-wire of capabilities dropped in the React-Query migration). Delete and bulk delete are gated to admin/org-admin behind `ConfirmationModal` "cannot be undone" copy; provider self-assign ("Assign to me") calls `assignTicket`. Status transition remains service inventory only. Restored writes flow through the optimistic `useSupportTicketsMutations` lifecycle (`applyOptimisticRemove` for delete, `applyOptimisticUpsert` for assign) that converges on the `['support']` cache.
- `MobileSupportTickets.jsx` preserves pull-to-refresh, load more, KPI choices, search, filter, analytics trigger, row reveal, details, and safe edit. It no longer renders fake chart data, `LIVE` trend copy, bulk selection state, or local-only filtered row truth. The detail sheet exposes provider self-assign and a role-gated single delete (confirmed on the page via `ConfirmationModal`).
- The page is admitted for guarded continuation, but it does not promote support-specific visuals, stats, or actions into global canon.

Support preservation ledger:

| Old Support behavior | Intake decision | Evidence path |
|---|---|---|
| Route `/support-tickets`, Help rail entry, and Support nav label. | Preserve route and simple user-facing label. Verify sponsor exclusion through `ProtectedRoute` nav access before admission. | `App.js`, `routes.jsx`, `navigation.js`, `consoleModuleRail.js`, `ProtectedRoute.jsx` |
| Provider-facing `My Requests` title and provider own-ticket filtering. | Preserve the provider/simple support request experience as `My support`; move owner filtering into the canonical service projection. | `SupportTicketsPage.jsx`, `supportTicketsService.js` |
| Admin/org-admin support queue with grid/list/table modes and view toggle. | Preserve as old behavior inventory. Convert active UI to one Requests-style handled sheet; legacy density surfaces stay inactive until re-audited. | `SupportTicketsPage.jsx`, `SupportTicketListView.jsx`, `SupportTicketTableView.jsx` |
| KPI cards, status choices, and analytics summary. | Converted into one support signal plus service-backed state choices and scoped stats. Analytics reveal uses the scoped route stats until a richer receiver is proved. | `SupportTicketsPage.jsx`, `MobileSupportTickets.jsx`, `supportTicketsService.js` |
| Filter sheet and search. | Preserved through shared `FilterSheet`; search/filter changes return to the route-owned query. | `SupportTicketsPage.jsx`, `FilterSheet.jsx` |
| Create and edit ticket modal paths. | Preserved through shared `ModalShell`; active form payload is narrowed to request text/classification only. | `SupportTicketModal.jsx`, `supportTicketsService.js` |

Stats stays unavailable for roles without support management scope. | Mobile pull-to-refresh, local KPI/search filter, row reveal, selection, and actions. | Preserve refresh/load-more/reveal/details/edit inventory. Convert fake/local charts, uppercase noise, selection/delete/assign actions, and local-only count truth before mobile admission. | `MobileSupportTickets.jsx` |

### Page 11 Admission - Health News

Audit status: started on 2026-07-03 and admitted for guarded read-only continuation after source, schema/RLS authoring block, responsive Browser proof, shell command cleanup, hardgate, build, encoding, and local cleanup. Decision: Health News at `/health-news` is admitted under the Today/Requests canon as a read-only published-feed surface only. This does not make Health News a reusable global pattern source, and it does not admit create, edit, publish, delete, import, or notification side effects.

Route and RBAC:

- `App.js` mounts `/health-news` behind `ProtectedRoute minRole="org_admin"`.
- `routes.jsx` names `/health-news` as `minRole: 'org_admin'`, `resource: 'news'`, and title `Health News`.
- `navigation.js` exposes Health News under Admin as `minRole: 'org_admin'`.
- Providers, sponsors, and viewers should not receive the route or nav item. Admin and org-admin can access it.

HEAD snapshot evidence for this ledger: `git show HEAD:frontend/src/components/pages/HealthNewsManagementPage.jsx` and `git show HEAD:frontend/src/services/healthNewsService.js` show the old/current behavior inventory: `usePageFooter`, `useViewMode('health-news-page', 'table')`, `ViewToggle`, `FilterSheet`, `AnalyticsModal`, `HealthNewsModal`, `ConfirmationModal`, `BulkActionBar`, `HealthNewsListView`, `HealthNewsTableView`, `MobileHealthNews`, pagination, `openHealthNewsModal`, `openAnalyticsModal`, direct `health_news` reads, create/edit/delete, publish toggle, bulk import, analytics, and realtime subscription.

Current source owner and conversion state:

- Source conversion started on 2026-07-03. `healthNewsService.js` now exposes `getHealthNewsPage()` and `getHealthNewsPageStats()` as the route projection for the proved curated published feed, with exact count, search, category/source/date filters, pagination, URL normalization, and `scope: 'published_feed'`.
- Writer/content proof was checked against the current schema and alignment docs. `health_news` persists only `id`, `title`, `source`, `url`, `image_url`, `category`, `published`, and `created_at`; deployed/live confirmation says `description`, `content`, and `icon` are absent; the policy matrix proves public published read only. This contradicts re-enabling create/edit/publish/delete from Console.
- `HealthNewsManagementPage.jsx` no longer performs page-level direct `supabase.from('health_news')` reads, no longer applies a mismatched page-level `applyAuthFilter`, and no longer imports content write, publish, delete, notification, confirmation, or bulk action handlers.
- Route read failure now renders `Health news could not load. Try again.` and clears count/stats instead of silently presenting failed truth as an empty published feed.
- Active create, edit, publish/unpublish, single delete, bulk delete, and notification side effects are unavailable/excluded from the active page until content writer, role authority, RLS, confirmation, and app consequence proof exist.
- Shell command cleanup now treats `/health-news` as route-owned in `ContextAwareFAB.jsx` and `DynamicBottomBar.jsx`; those global shells no longer import or open `HealthNewsModal` for `mode="create"`. `useContextAction.js` routes the Health News action to `openHealthNewsModal`, where the page shows unavailable feedback, instead of `openModal('healthNews')`.
- `MobileHealthNews.jsx` preserves pull-to-refresh, load more, search/filter intent, analytics, row reveal, and details. Mobile projection cleanup on 2026-07-04 removed duplicate local search/KPI row filtering; mobile now renders the page-provided `getHealthNewsPage()` projection directly while search and KPI taps write route filters. It no longer renders hardcoded chart data, `LIVE` trend copy, row selection, publish/unpublish, edit, or delete controls.
- `HealthNewsManagementPage.jsx` now publishes a route-owned `healthNewsPanelContext`, `ContextPanel.jsx` listens for `healthNewsRouteContextUpdated`, and `HealthNewsPanel.jsx` renders that projection instead of starting a second feed request. The panel no longer performs duplicate direct `supabase.from('health_news')` reads or exposes Create/Preview/Export actions.
- `HealthNewsListView.jsx` and `HealthNewsTableView.jsx` are read/details-only density surfaces. Active edit, publish/unpublish, delete, selection, and admin command props are no longer passed from the page or declared by those density views.
- `HealthNewsModal.jsx` now uses shared `ModalShell` for dialog chrome. Active page behavior opens a read-only `HealthNewsReadView` for details; create/edit writer authority, payload fields, RLS, and app/public-feed consequence remain unproved and unavailable from the active page.
- Dormant `useHealthNews.js` realtime now refreshes without logging row payloads to the browser console.
- `HealthNewsManagementPage.jsx` now uses `usePageShell({ bleed: true, hideFab: true })`, disables the private footer with `usePageFooter(null, 'status', false)`, and renders a Requests-style signal, state choices, handled sheet, sheet-owned pagination, and focused detail rail while preserving old grid/list/table density inventory.
- `MobileHealthNews.jsx` now exposes canonical mobile proof hooks for the mobile activity sheet, mobile search, and mobile failed-load state.
- Shared `FilterSheet.jsx` now exposes `role="dialog"`, `aria-modal="true"`, `mobile-filter-sheet`, and `filter-sheet` hooks so filter drawers are part of the canonical reveal system instead of anonymous floating surfaces.
- The active Health News surfaces are now in the default UI hardgate: `HealthNewsManagementPage.jsx`, `MobileHealthNews.jsx`, `HealthNewsPanel.jsx`, `HealthNewsListView.jsx`, `HealthNewsTableView.jsx`, and `HealthNewsModal.jsx`.
- Source/build proof passed on 2026-07-03: `cmd /c node scripts/check-ui-surface-hardgate.js src/components/common/FilterSheet.jsx src/components/mobile/MobileHealthNews.jsx src/components/modals/HealthNewsModal.jsx`; `cmd /c npm test -- --watchAll=false HealthNewsManagementPage.contract.test.js PageRevampGate.contract.test.js` passed 13 tests; and `cmd /c npm run build` passed with bundle `main.986b4aa5.js`.
- Rendered Browser/IAB proof reused one static production server on `127.0.0.1:3000`: desktop `1280x900` rendered `Health News | iVisit Console`, one handled sheet, table density, sheet search, focused detail rail, no horizontal overflow, no stale `Health News Management`, `Total Articles`, `FILTERED`, `VIEW ALL`, `LAST 7 DAYS`, `No News Articles Yet`, `LIVE`, or active `Delete` copy, and clean console logs. The `Recent` state tap selected the state and settled to the empty recent feed without stale rows. The detail trigger opened one dialog with the read-only note, zero form inputs, no horizontal overflow, and clean console logs. Tablet `834x1112` kept the sheet and table, hid the right rail, avoided mobile bottom-nav leakage, and had no horizontal overflow or console warnings/errors. Mobile `390x844` rendered the mobile activity sheet/search, avatar/account entry, bottom island, no desktop sheet/rail, no stale unsafe copy, no horizontal overflow, and clean console logs. The mobile filter tap opened one `role="dialog"`/`aria-modal="true"` bottom sheet with one close control, no overflow, and clean logs.

Create, edit, publish, delete, import, export, preview, and notification side effects remain unavailable until authoring authority is proved.

Health News preservation ledger:

| Old Health News behavior | Intake decision | Evidence path |
|---|---|---|
| Route `/health-news` and Admin nav label `Health News`. | Preserve the route and simple user-facing label; keep org-admin/admin access only unless a product decision changes sponsor/content ownership. | `App.js`, `routes.jsx`, `navigation.js`, `ProtectedRoute.jsx` |
| Direct page list/count/stat reads from `health_news`. | Converted into `getHealthNewsPage()` for the proved published-feed projection. Context panel now renders the route-owned projection instead of starting a second feed request. | `HealthNewsManagementPage.jsx`, `healthNewsService.js`, `ContextPanel.jsx`, `HealthNewsPanel.jsx` |
| Grid/list/table view modes and `ViewToggle`. | Preserved as density variants inside one handled Health News sheet. List and table density views are read/details-only and hardgate-clean. | `HealthNewsManagementPage.jsx`, `HealthNewsListView.jsx`, `HealthNewsTableView.jsx` |
| KPI cards, published/draft/medical/recent choices, search, and filters. | Converted into a Health News signal, service-backed state choices, inline sheet search, shared filter reveal, and sheet-owned count/page copy. | `HealthNewsManagementPage.jsx`, `MobileHealthNews.jsx`, `healthNewsService.js` |
| Create and edit article modal paths. | Modal chrome is converted to shared `ModalShell`, and active details now render a read-only source review. Active create/edit remain unavailable until simple copy, scoped payload fields, pending state, receiver/RLS proof, and public-feed consequence are proved. | `HealthNewsManagementPage.jsx`, `HealthNewsModal.jsx`, `healthNewsService.js` |
| Publish/unpublish workflow and notification side effect. | Excluded from active UI until content workflow authority, notification receiver, role scope, confirmation copy, and app consequence are proved. | `HealthNewsManagementPage.jsx`, `healthNewsService.js`, `notificationService.js` |

| Bulk import service inventory. | Keep as service inventory only until import source, validation, duplicate handling, role authority, and app/public feed consequence are proved. | `healthNewsService.js` |

Global desktop/mobile shells do not import or mount `HealthNewsModal` create mode, and generic Health News action dispatches the page-owned event. | `ContextAwareFAB.jsx`, `DynamicBottomBar.jsx`, `useContextAction.js`, `HealthNewsManagementPage.jsx` |

Fake charts, `LIVE` copy, ratio/pulse/registry dashboard wording, local-only row filtering, selection, publish, edit, and delete are removed from active mobile; mobile now has an explicit failed-load state and proof hooks. | `MobileHealthNews.jsx`, `FilterSheet.jsx` |
| Realtime subscription service inventory. | Preserve only as route-owned refresh with cleanup after scope and duplicate startup ownership are named. Dormant hook no longer logs row payloads. | `healthNewsService.js`, `useHealthNews.js` |

Decision: Health News is admitted as a deliberately read-only published-feed surface. Current schema/RLS evidence blocks authoring, so active create/edit/publish/delete/import/notification behavior cannot return until a backend content-authoring pass adds receiver fields, role authority, payload validation, RLS expectation, and app/public-feed consequence. This admits Page 11 only; it does not promote Health News visuals, feed stats, or content actions into global canon.

### Page 12 Intake Audit - Insurance

Audit status: started on 2026-07-03. Decision: Insurance at `/insurance` is in intake only and is not admitted under the Today/Requests canon. No visual revamp, shared Requests pattern reuse, or command re-enable is authorized yet.

Route and RBAC:

- `App.js` mounts `/insurance` behind `ProtectedRoute minRole="admin"`.
- `routes.jsx` names `/insurance` as `minRole: 'admin'`, `resource: 'insurance'`, and title `Insurance`.
- `navigation.js` exposes Insurance under Payments as `minRole: 'admin'`.
- `ContextPanel.jsx` also gates the route with `isAdmin()`.
- Org admins, providers, sponsors, and viewers should not receive the Insurance management route unless a later product and backend authority decision changes the route.

HEAD snapshot evidence for this ledger: `git show HEAD:frontend/src/components/pages/InsuranceManagementPage.jsx`, `git show HEAD:frontend/src/services/insuranceService.js`, and `git show HEAD:frontend/src/hooks/useInsurance.js` at current `HEAD` commit `f31f29f` showed no Insurance-specific commits in the last seven days and confirmed the old/current behavior baseline: `usePageHeader`, `usePageFooter`, `useInsurance`, `useViewMode('insurance', 'grid')`, `ViewToggle`, five KPI cards, `FilterSheet`, `AnalyticsModal`, `InsuranceModal`, `ConfirmationModal`, `BulkActionBar`, grid/list/table density views, `MobileInsurance`, local pagination, search/status/type/verification/date filters, `openInsuranceModal`, `openFilters`, `openAnalyticsModal`, create/edit/view/delete/verify/bulk delete handlers, analytics summary, private card image upload, and realtime subscription.

Required before Page 12 can move past intake:

1. Keep `getInsurancePage()` as the active route projection owner for policy list/detail intake, exact count, scoped stats, explicit failed/denied/empty states, stale-refresh degraded feedback, and guarded realtime cleanup.
2. Decide whether admin policy management is allowed through RLS, RPC, or a backend command. Until then, create/edit/delete/status/verify exports and UI affordances stay unavailable.
3. Keep billing-result visibility read-only through `getInsuranceBillingOutcomes()` until exception handling, settlement actions, and billing mutation authority are separately proved; Console must not invent an `insurance_billing` mutation path from trigger-created evidence.
4. Keep insurance card upload and direct card image previews disabled until Storage path, owner, expiry, cleanup, and URL regeneration are proved against the app's `insurance/${user.id}/...` object-key contract; the compatibility export must continue to fail closed until then.
5. Keep hidden insurance data acquisition closed in global FAB, mobile bottom action, unrelated `PageDataContext` startup domains, and right-panel handoffs. The right panel may consume the route policy and billing context; it must not start its own policy list read, billing summary read, policy realtime channel, or billing realtime channel while `/insurance` is mounted.
6. Keep detail modal chrome on the canonical modal system and keep command fields read-only until rendered proof can run.
7. Keep mobile metrics tied to scoped route stats or honest unavailable state; do not restore fake live/chart language.
8. Keep mobile policy rows read-only. Do not restore mobile edit/delete/verify, selection, or bulk props/buttons until admin policy command authority, receiver ownership, payload contract, authorization expectation, and app consequence are proved.
9. Keep legacy adapter reads/realtime/coverage helpers and legacy service/hook list/detail reads closed unless a caller-specific receiver, projection, role scope, and app consequence are proved.
10. Keep `buildInsuranceWritePayload()` fail-closed until an admin policy command receiver and app consequence are proved.
11. Add Page 12 hardgate coverage only when visual implementation starts.

Insurance Requests-canon blocker map:

This is a blocker map, not a design target. Page 12 may not reuse Requests visual language until each slot below is proved, adapted, excluded, or marked legacy inactive from Insurance's own old behavior.

| Requests canon slot | Insurance current evidence | Page 12 admission decision |
|---|---|---|
| Signal field | `getInsurancePage()` now owns policy rows, exact count, scoped stats, denied state, failed state, and stale-refresh degraded state, but the active UI still presents the old management/KPI opener. | Not admitted. A future pass must replace the opener with one insurance signal and prove denied, failed, empty, partial, and complete states without fake clear truth. |
| State choices | Old total/active/pending/expired/unverified KPIs and mobile metric chips are preserved as inventory; current stats are scoped but type/date taxonomy still needs product proof. | Not admitted. Future choices must map to service filters and exact counts, with visible selected/pressed state. |
| Handled sheet | Grid/list/table density modes, pagination, search, and filters remain represented; route reads are now service-owned. | Intake only. A future handled sheet must classify each density mode as preserved, converted, excluded, or legacy inactive and stay on one projection. |
| Focused detail | `InsuranceModal.jsx` uses shared `ModalShell` and is read-only, with policy evidence, linked billing outcomes, and card-reference acknowledgement without direct signed-URL image preview. | Partially safe as evidence only. It is not the final focus rail/detail pattern until edit/upload/verify/delete authority is either proved or intentionally excluded in the visual pass. |
| Route-owned action | Header Add, edit, delete, verify, bulk delete, export, and global actions now route to unavailable feedback or are hidden; right-panel Add and Export are muted and marked unavailable instead of primary/live. | Blocked. No route action may be enabled until role gate, receiver owner, payload, pending state, success/failure feedback, and `source truth -> receiver -> app consequence` are proved. |
| Shared modal/sheet | `InsuranceModal` uses `ModalShell`; `FilterSheet` and `AnalyticsModal` remain as old secondary surfaces. | Intake only. The visual pass must prove shared modal/filter/analytics semantics, mobile chrome suppression, and no private backdrop/filter/dropdown chrome. |
| Mobile recomposition | `MobileInsurance` keeps pull-to-refresh, load more, search, filters, analytics, row reveal, scoped stats, and read-only Details, and removes fake `LIVE`/chart data, local-only visible-row filtering, plus mobile edit/delete/verify and selection controls. | Not admitted. Mobile still needs the Requests rhythm: signal first, compact choices, handled sheet, row reveal, route action only if allowed, and no destructive controls without proof. |
| Data quieting | Global FAB/bottom action imports are removed, `/insurance` is route-owned, PageData startup no longer loads insurance by default, the remaining lazy/manual PageData read now uses `getInsurancePage()`, mobile search/KPI controls write route filters instead of filtering a partial visible list, route reads/realtime refresh use stale-result/unmount guards, right-panel policy rows/stats and billing rows/stats now come from `insurancePanelContext`, legacy duplicate read/realtime/coverage helpers fail closed, legacy service/hook list/detail reads fail closed without auto-fetch or auto-subscription, legacy duplicate write export names delegate to fail-closed `insuranceService.js` commands, legacy payload construction fails closed, legacy `useInsurance` commands fail closed before local mutation, and the old card-upload export fails closed instead of writing Storage. | Partially proved. Storage path ownership for re-enabling uploads, admin policy command authority, and billing command ownership remain blockers. |
| Local semantic color | Current active page still contains old card geometry and some semantic destructive/ring patterns and is not in the default visual hardgate; right-panel loading plus ordinary policy evidence, verified marks, filter feedback, mobile details, list/table icons, and modal evidence chrome have moved off brand-primary. | Not admitted. Future colors must reserve red for failed/destructive/expired meanings and keep ordinary filters, selection, and empty recovery neutral or local semantic. |
| Interaction feedback | Unavailable command feedback exists through toasts, loading/error states exist, and mobile row reveal remains. | Intake only. Future visual work must prove pressed, selected, opening, pending, disabled, retry, pagination, modal trigger, and route-transition feedback in rendered desktop and mobile proof. |

Promotion rule: the first Insurance visual pass must close this blocker map before adding Page 12 to the default hardgate. Until then, Insurance remains a safety/data-ownership intake surface, not a revamped Requests-style page.

Decision: Insurance is Page 12 intake only. It is not admitted, not a reusable pattern source, and not ready for visual implementation. The next safe implementation slice is authority and data ownership cleanup, not a UI rebuild.

- `TodayHome.jsx` still uses `usePageShell({ bleed: true, hideFab: true })`, `usePageHeader('Today')`, a hidden footer, role-scoped module rail data, clickable first-glance cards, route-opening feedback, and no page-private sidebar, footer, modal, notification dropdown, or mobile hamburger pattern.
- `EmergencyRequestsPage.jsx` still uses `usePageShell({ bleed: true, hideFab: true })`, `getEmergencyRequestsPage()`, exact-count pagination, route-owned realtime refresh for `emergency_requests` and `payments`, shared filter and analytics reveals, confirmation/retry modal paths, structural loading/empty/error states, and mobile `MobileEmergency` recomposition.
- `git show HEAD:frontend/src/components/pages/EmergencyRequestsPage.jsx` at `f31f29f` still confirms the old Requests behavior inventory: `ViewToggle`, list/table views, direct Supabase list/count reads, filter sheet, analytics modal, single and bulk cancel flows, cash modal, retry modal, pagination, realtime subscriptions, and mobile support.

Decision: Today remains the first-glance role-home canon. Requests remains the multi-data stage/sheet/detail canon. This authorizes only audited page-by-page continuation; it does not authorize copied markup, private chrome, or a page skipping old-behavior preservation.

## Gate Order

### 1. Authority And RBAC

Every page must name:

- Route path and minimum role.
- Navigation visibility by role.
- Visible actions by role.
- Disabled or unavailable actions by role.
- Backend owner for each action.
- Whether the action is read-only, ordinary CRUD, a workflow command, or not ready.
- Unauthorized state and copy.
- App consequence, if the action changes shared iVisit truth.

If an action cannot prove its backend owner, it must be hidden, disabled, or shown as `Not ready yet`.

### 2. Layout Relationship

Every page must account for:

- `AppShell` padding, full-bleed mode, and scroll ownership.
- `SmartHeader` title and actions.
- `IslandNavigation` desktop behavior.
- `DynamicBottomBar` mobile behavior.
- `SmartFooter` visibility.
- `ContextAwareFAB` visibility.
- `ContextPanelShell`, modals, sheets, and z-index relationships.
- Safe areas, mobile height, tablet width, desktop width, and wide desktop width.
- Whether the page owns a sheet, side panel, canvas, table, or card grid.

No page is complete if the design only works inside a decorative container.

### 3. Product Language

UI-facing text must stay simple:

- Prefer `Today`, `Needs attention`, `Requests`, `Approvals`, `Visits`, `Staff`, `Payments`, `Help`, `Details`, and `Not ready yet`.
- Avoid internal words such as `receiver`, `source truth`, `workflow command`, `audit`, `dispatch`, and `operations`.
- Keep one dominant action per moment.
- Use short reasons only after reveal, tap, hover, or expansion.

### 4. iVisit Design Fit

Every page must feel like the same ecosystem as `ivisit-app`:

- Calm first screen.
- Sheet-first interaction.
- Progressive reveal.
- Theme-aware surfaces.
- Responsive recomposition, not desktop shrink.
- Immediate pressed, pending, loading, disabled, or route feedback.
- No fake metrics or theatrical enterprise copy.

### 5. Surface Noise Hardgate

Active revamp pages must not use decorative borders, rings, dividers, outlines, or pixel hairlines.

Run:

`npm run check:ui-hardgate`

The gate currently checks:

- `src/components/pages/TodayHome.jsx`
- `src/components/common/ProtectedRoute.jsx`
- `src/components/common/IslandNavigation.jsx`
- `src/components/navigation/DynamicBottomBar.jsx`
- `src/components/navigation/MobileNavMenu.jsx`
- `src/components/ui/theme-toggle.jsx`
- `src/components/common/ConsoleModuleRail.jsx`
- `src/config/consoleModuleRail.js`
- `src/config/mobileNavigation.js`
- `src/components/pages/EmergencyRequestsPage.jsx`
- `src/components/mobile/MobileEmergency.jsx`
- `src/components/pages/VerificationQueue.jsx`
- `src/components/mobile/MobileVerification.jsx`
- `src/components/views/VerificationQueueListView.jsx`
- `src/components/views/VerificationQueueTableView.jsx`
- `src/components/modals/VerificationModal.jsx`
- `src/components/pages/DoctorsPage.jsx`
- `src/components/mobile/MobileDoctors.jsx`
- `src/components/views/DoctorListView.jsx`
- `src/components/views/DoctorTableView.jsx`
- `src/components/modals/DoctorModal.jsx`
- `src/components/pages/WalletManagementPage.jsx`
- `src/components/mobile/MobileWallet.jsx`
- `src/components/context/WalletPanel.jsx`
- `src/components/modals/GlobalFinancialModals.jsx`

As each page enters the revamp, add that page to `scripts/check-ui-surface-hardgate.js` before the page can be considered active. Old legacy pages may still contain borders and rings until their own page pass starts.

Allowed replacements:

- Spacing.
- Surface depth.
- Color blocking.
- Backdrop blur.
- Typography contrast.
- Motion and reveal states.

Do not replace a border with a fake one-pixel shadow, a `h-px` divider, or a literal `1px` CSS hairline.

### 6. Implementation Scope

Code work must stay page-scoped unless the page proves a shell, route, nav, data, or RBAC dependency is blocking it.

Shared changes are allowed only when they make the page truthful:

- Route config.
- Navigation config.
- Layout context.
- Shared unauthorized state.
- Shared data loading boundaries.

### 7. QA Proof

Before moving to the next page, record:

- Build result.
- UI hardgate result.
- Encoding/mojibake checks for touched text files.
- Desktop screenshot.
- Mobile screenshot.
- Light and dark theme check.
- Interaction proof for primary action and reveal states.
- RBAC proof for allowed, limited, and blocked roles.
- Known follow-up risks.

## Page 1 Ledger: Today Home

Path: `/`

Current decision:

- The route is `Today`, not `Dashboard`.
- The page is protected at `viewer` and above.
- Console patients should not use this as the patient product surface.
- Non-patient console roles render `TodayHome`.
- High-risk work is represented as navigation, read-only evidence, or `Not ready yet`.

RBAC proof:

- `ROUTE_PROTECTION['/']` is not public and requires `viewer`.
- `NAV_CONFIG.main.home` is labeled `Today` and requires `viewer`.
- Mobile bottom nav no longer sends viewers to `Health News`; viewers only get `Today` and `Settings`.
- Mobile bottom nav gives org admins `Approvals` and `Staff`, providers `Emergencies` and `Visits`, and sponsors `Statistics` and `Settings`.
- The verification route title and nav label now use `Approvals`, not `Queue` or `Pending Approvals`.
- `Health News` is locked as an org-admin management route across mounted routes, route config, and navigation config; provider users do not get the management surface.
- Sponsors are explicitly excluded from provider-operation routes in route utilities and navigation config: `Live Map`, `Emergencies`, `Visits`, and `Support` no longer appear as sponsor-accessible routes through the numeric role hierarchy.
- `ProtectedRoute` still performs auth, profile, onboarding, navigation, min-role, role-list, and resource checks.
- The Today rail filters its destinations by explicit role slots, not inherited numeric role level.
- Sponsor desktop rail access is limited to `Today` and `Statistics`; it does not inherit provider rail destinations such as `Requests` or `Help`.
- Startup data fetches and realtime listeners in `PageDataProvider` are role-gated before loading provider, org, or admin domains.
- `PageDataProvider` uses an explicit role access contract, not numeric role inheritance, so sponsors can load analytics without loading provider emergency, visit, support, or activity domains.
- Provider Today startup no longer loads global analytics; the `Statistics` page owns provider analytics loading when the provider opens that route.
- `PageDataProvider` startup domains are explicit by role and do not include `user_activity`; activity remains a lazy/manual fetch surface so Today does not log feed errors it does not render.
- Global PageData analytics now requests summary metrics only; raw chart arrays remain a page-level analytics concern.
- Unauthorized copy now says the role cannot open the page, without control-room language.

Layout proof:

- `TodayHome` owns full-bleed mode through `usePageShell({ bleed: true, hideFab: true })`.
- The global FAB is hidden on Today.
- The mobile bottom-bar plus action also respects `hideFab`, leaving Today with one dominant action.
- The SmartFooter is hidden on Today.
- The SmartHeader remains active and names the page `Today`.
- Parent `BentoHome` header and footer effects no longer override the Today shell for console roles.
- The mobile nav sheet has hidden title/description text, named icon controls, and no hairline dividers.
- The desktop rail stays inside the page surface.
- The desktop rail no longer uses inherited provider-level access for sponsors.
- The desktop island navigation is now part of the Today hardgate.
- The desktop island avatar uses a local role-toned fallback instead of direct provider avatar URLs.
- The Today sheet is a side sheet on large screens and a bottom sheet on mobile.
- The mobile bottom nav centers when Today hides the action FAB and sits in its own glass surface.
- Mobile canonical navigation is now: avatar opens account/overflow sheet, bottom island holds only role-primary destinations, and the FAB appears only for one page-level action.
- The redundant bottom-island menu icon was removed so the avatar remains the only mobile sheet opener.
- The mobile action list uses a compact tray so visible rows are not covered by the bottom nav.
- The page avoids an outer container and owns its atlas/canvas background.
- Bleed pages use the shell `no-scrollbar` class instead of the red custom scrollbar, so Today does not show a vertical hairline on desktop.
- Viewer navigation does not expose `Live Map`; that item now requires `provider`.
- Decorative rings, borders, and dot halos were removed from the active Today surface.
- CSS atlas hairlines were removed from the active Today surface.
- Standalone atlas waypoint dots were removed from the active Today background so the page relies on quiet depth and motion, not decorative markers.

State proof:

- First glance now shows role-relevant status chips instead of generic page labels.
- The sheet rows are role-specific action items.
- Role-specific Today state is covered by `TodayHome.contract.test.js` for admin, org admin, provider, sponsor, viewer, and live-unavailable states.
- `Activity`, `Details`, and generic `Not ready yet` placeholder rows were removed.
- Row-level secondary actions only appear after reveal, and rows start collapsed.
- Emergency, approval, visit, sponsor, viewer, and live-unavailable states have direct action copy.
- Sponsor request activity is now read-only evidence on Today, not a secondary action into the emergency operations page.
- The primary action still remains the obvious first action.
- Mobile first-glance chips are compact and avoid text clipping.
- Mobile admin first glance shows three clean rows, with the fourth row revealed by tray scroll instead of being clipped by navigation.
- Desktop rail icon buttons have accessible labels.
- Desktop rail icon buttons now have focus-visible feedback, an `opening` state, and spinner icon mutation during route handoff.
- First-glance cards are semantic buttons with CTA icons, pointer hover, keyboard focus, tap/press feedback, and an `opening` state before route navigation.
- Requests opens `/emergencies`, Approvals opens `/verification`, and Staff opens `/doctors` for admin and org-admin roles.
- The CTA icon mutates from the arrow to a spinner while the destination route is opening.
- Smart island navigation now opens on hover or keyboard focus and collapses when focus leaves, so it does not stay over Today while a glance card is active.
- The no-access state was simplified to plain role feedback and direct actions.
- Decorative no-access background effects and top stripe were removed from the active pass.
- Role-critical domain failures now make Today render `Live details are not ready` instead of turning failed reads into false `Clear` counts.
- Today-critical startup reads for visits, help, approvals, staff, and sponsor analytics now set explicit domain errors instead of mock fallback counts when they fail.
- Today startup service fan-out now uses quiet read paths for analytics, hospitals, ambulances, users, insurance, organizations, profile display-id enrichment, and subscriber startup fetches, so raw backend payloads do not leak into the browser console before Today can render the visible degraded state.
- Route identity now has an app-owned `SEOHead` document-title fallback, so Today resolves to `Today | iVisit Console` even if Helmet does not apply the head update in the live browser.

Page 1 exit decision:

- Today/Home may hand off to Page 2 implementation under a documented proof block: authenticated sponsor screenshot proof cannot be produced in this Supabase project without creating or mutating an account, and read-only inventory found 0 sponsor accounts. Sponsor behavior remains covered by route/nav/render contracts until a real sponsor credential exists.
- Do not call Page 1 release-complete until either a real sponsor credential is available and screenshot proof is added, or product explicitly removes sponsor screenshot proof from the Page 1 release gate.

Next page rule:

Do not start page 2 until Today has a fresh build, encoding checks, and at least one role-authenticated browser pass or a documented reason that credentials are unavailable.

## Page 2 Intake: Requests

Path: `/emergencies`

Current decision:

- The page is surfaced to users as `Requests`; the canonical URL remains `/emergencies` for route compatibility.
- The route is protected at `provider` and above, excluding `sponsor`.
- Sponsors must receive blocked-route proof for `/emergencies`; they do not need a Requests page screenshot because Requests is not a sponsor surface.
- The page is high-risk because it can change emergency lifecycle, responder assignment, payment retry, cash approval, and visit/clinical consequences.

RBAC and route proof to preserve:

- `src/App.js` mounts `/emergencies` behind `<ProtectedRoute minRole="provider">`.
- `ROUTE_PROTECTION['/emergencies']` requires `provider`, resource `emergency_requests`, and excludes sponsors.
- `NAV_CONFIG.ops.items` exposes `Requests` to providers, org admins, and admins, and excludes sponsors.
- `TodayHome` only links admin/org-admin/provider Requests cards and rails to `/emergencies`; sponsor request activity stays in `Impact`.

Backend owner inventory:

- Read owner is now `src/services/emergencyService.js` through `getEmergencyRequestsPage`, which owns emergency request count, list, role scope, payment enrichment, and row normalization for the active page.
- Dispatch owner is the `console_dispatch_emergency` RPC, reached through `emergencyService.dispatchEmergency` or `emergencyResponseService.dispatchEmergency`.
- Bed-flow accept/update owner is `console_update_emergency_request`.
- Complete owner is `console_complete_emergency`.
- Cancel owner is `console_cancel_emergency`.
- Payment retry owner is `retry_payment_with_different_method`.
- Cash approval/decline owners are `approve_cash_payment` and `decline_cash_payment`.
- Manual cash settlement remains blocked on the finance receiver pass; do not re-enable browser-side `walletService.processCashPayment` as an ordinary page action.

Current risks found before implementation:

- The first Page 2 implementation pass moved `EmergencyRequestsPage` list/count/payment enrichment into `getEmergencyRequestsPage` in `emergencyService`, so the active page no longer performs direct `supabase.from('emergency_requests')` or `supabase.from('payments')` reads.
- The first visual interaction pass replaced the active desktop grid/table render with a Requests workspace: filter cards, scan list, and a right-side request detail rail with one primary action.
- The active mobile Requests surface now uses plain Requests copy, live counts, compact filters, expanding rows, and no synthetic trend chart data.

Implementation order:

1. Close the read-owner gap by moving count/list/payment enrichment into a service or query hook with an explicit projection and quiet failure behavior.
2. Add Page 2 files to the UI hardgate before visual completion, then remove borders/rings/hairlines only inside the active Requests surface.
3. Replace hidden hover-only row actions with visible action menus or progressive row reveal that works on pointer, keyboard, and touch.
4. Remove native `window.prompt`/`window.confirm` paths and keep unsupported cash settlement unavailable with honest copy.
5. Rebuild desktop and mobile layouts from the approved Requests concept, preserving one dominant action and a sheet/details-first interaction model.
6. Prove RBAC with admin, org admin, provider, and sponsor-blocked route checks before declaring Page 2 complete.

## Page 3: Approvals

Admission recheck, 2026-06-25:

- Decision: Approvals is re-admitted for guarded continuation under the Today/Requests canon. It may keep moving as Page 3 because its old behavior has been represented, active source owners are named, and current contract tests cover the main RBAC/layout/data gates. It does not become a new global layout canonical; Today remains the first-glance reference and Requests remains the multi-data reference.
- Route and role: `/verification`, minimum `org_admin`. Admin can review and command. Org-admin can open the page and review scoped visible records, but does not receive approve/reject, modal approval, bulk selection, or facility command affordances.
- Page-to-shell relationship: normal/table workspace inside the shared app shell. `VerificationQueue.jsx` supplies `usePageHeader("Approvals")`, disables the floating footer with `usePageFooter(null, 'status', false)`, uses shared filter/view controls, relies on route-owned FAB suppression for `/verification`, and relies on shared mobile bottom island slots.
- Data owner: provider approval reads are `verificationService.getVerificationQueue()`. Facility approval reads are `orgVerificationService.getOrgVerificationQueue()`. PageData startup fan-out returns `[]` on `/verification`, so the route owns these reads.
- Command owner: provider approval/rejection is `verificationService.verifyProvider()` and remains admin-only. Facility approval/rejection is `orgVerificationService.verifyOrganization()` and remains admin-only. UI `approved` maps to stored facility `verified`.
- Responsive owner: desktop stays provider/facility tabs with grid/list/table variants where useful; mobile uses `MobileVerification` with pull-to-refresh, KPI strip, Provider/Facility segmented control, compact search/filter/analytics triggers, row reveal, and admin-only row commands.
- Today/Requests fit: Approvals reuses the canon's simple copy, state choices, local non-danger colors, immediate button feedback, route-owned data, empty/loading/error discipline, and mobile recomposition. It intentionally does not force a Requests detail rail because the old page's approval work depends on multi-mode review and provider/facility lanes.

- `cmd /c npm run check:ui-hardgate` passed for 25 active files.
- `git diff --check` passed for the touched gate/roadmap docs.
- Mojibake scan found no matches in the touched gate/roadmap docs. Non-ASCII review found intentional arrows, dashes, box drawing, and status symbols already used by the planning docs.

Old `HEAD` behavior inventory and active decision:

| Provider/person lane read `profiles` where `role = provider`, with BVN status as the visible queue field. | Preserved through `getVerificationQueue()` with page-owned filters, pagination, stats, and quiet reads. | `verificationService.js`, `VerificationQueue.jsx` |

| Admin-style permission check could leave non-command users in weak or empty states. | Converted into split authority: `canReview = admin || org_admin`, `canApprove = admin`; scoped empty copy for org-admin. | `VerificationQueue.jsx`, `VerificationQueue.contract.test.js` |
| Provider and facility approve/reject commands were visible through page, modal, mobile, table, and bulk paths. | Preserved only for admin; removed for org-admin and lower roles. | `VerificationQueue.jsx`, `MobileVerification.jsx`, `VerificationModal.jsx` |
| Grid/list/table modes and `ViewToggle` existed. | Preserved for desktop because approval review benefits from scan modes; not promoted as a universal canon. | `VerificationQueue.jsx`, `VerificationQueueListView.jsx`, `VerificationQueueTableView.jsx` |
| KPI/status cards filtered pending/approved/rejected/all. | Preserved as state choice buttons with local amber/emerald/red/sky meaning. | `VerificationQueue.jsx`, `MobileVerification.jsx` |
| Search and filter sheet existed. | Preserved with shell header trigger and mobile sheet trigger. Search/status intent flows into the route services; mobile renders the page-owned queue slice instead of applying a second local filter. | `VerificationQueue.jsx`, `FilterSheet`, `MobileVerification.jsx` |
| Analytics modal opened as a secondary surface. | Preserved as a secondary reveal, not first-screen chrome. | `VerificationQueue.jsx`, `MobileVerification.jsx` |
| Realtime subscriptions listened for provider and facility queue changes. | Preserved, but scoped to the active lane so inactive tabs do not own background truth. | `VerificationQueue.jsx`, `verificationService.js`, `orgVerificationService.js` |
| Pagination existed. | Preserved as page-body pagination and mobile pagination controls. | `VerificationQueue.jsx` |
| List/table delete callbacks existed but were inert. | Removed. Future destructive action requires receiver and app consequence proof. | `VerificationQueueListView.jsx`, `VerificationQueueTableView.jsx` |
| Bulk selection and bulk approve/reject existed. | Preserved for admin only; hidden for org-admin and lower roles. | `VerificationQueue.jsx`, `VerificationQueueTableView.jsx` |

| Modal used security/audit-heavy copy and global success/warning color tokens. | Converted to simpler `Review note` language, admin-only actions, and local amber/emerald/destructive tones. | `VerificationModal.jsx` |

| Loading, empty, unauthorized, and filtered states existed inconsistently. | Preserved and clarified with structural skeletons, access-restricted copy, scoped-empty org-admin copy, and filter recovery. | `VerificationQueue.jsx`, `MobileVerification.jsx` |

Current intake:

- Route: `/verification`.
- Route/nav authority: `App.js` protects it with `minRole="org_admin"`, `routes.jsx` titles it `Approvals`, and desktop/mobile nav labels it `Approvals`.
- Page owner: `VerificationQueue.jsx`.
- Responsive owner: `MobileVerification.jsx`.
- Provider/person read owner: `verificationService.getVerificationQueue()` reads `profiles` where `role = provider` and BVN status is the available queue field.
- Provider/person command owner: `verificationService.verifyProvider()` remains admin-only and writes `profiles.bvn_verified`; this is identity/BVN review, not dispatch certification.
- Facility read owner: `orgVerificationService.getOrgVerificationQueue()` reads `hospitals.verification_status`.
- Facility command owner: `orgVerificationService.verifyOrganization()` remains admin-only and writes `hospitals.verification_status` plus `hospitals.verified`; this affects facility readiness inputs, not broad organization approval.

First implementation proof:

- `VerificationQueue.jsx` now treats org-admin access as read/review access and admin as command access. Approve/reject buttons, modal approval actions, mobile approval actions, table selection, and bulk actions are only mounted for admins.
- The inert list/table Delete callback was removed from the Approvals parent path. A future destructive action needs an explicit receiver and reflected result before it can reappear.
- Realtime subscriptions are scoped to the active lane only: provider queue while the Providers tab is active, facility queue while the Facilities tab is active.
- Facility filters map UI `approved` to stored `verified`, preserving simple UI language while matching source truth.
- `/verification` is now route-owned in `pageDataAccess`, so `PageDataContext` does not also preload generic verification stats behind the route-owned queue.
- The global FAB and mobile bottom action are hidden synchronously on `/verification`, preventing closed-shell action chrome from mounting unrelated insurance or support hooks behind the route-owned page.
- `VerificationQueueTableView.jsx` only renders selection controls when selection callbacks exist, so non-admin roles do not receive inert bulk affordances.
- `verificationService.getVerificationQueue()` and `orgVerificationService.getOrgVerificationQueue()` now allow admin and org-admin reads; command functions remain admin-only.
- Mobile Approvals now receives the same command capability as desktop, removes loaded-row `LIVE` trend language, uses `Review Summary`, and labels the hospital-backed lane as `Facilities`.
- Mobile projection cleanup on 2026-07-04 removed duplicate local search/status filtering from `MobileVerification.jsx`; the mobile page now uses `useStableList(sourceItems, loading)` over the page-provided provider/facility queue slice while search, KPI, and filter controls still update the route-owned filters.
