## Page 4: Staff

Admission recheck, 2026-06-25:

- Decision: Staff is re-admitted for guarded continuation under the Today/Requests canon. It may proceed as the roster companion after Approvals, but it does not create a new global layout or action pattern.
- Route and RBAC: canonical path remains `/doctors`; user-facing label is `Staff`; route protection is `minRole: 'org_admin'`, `resource: 'doctors'`, and `title: 'Staff'`. Admin and org-admin can access/manage; provider, sponsor, and viewer do not get the route.
- Page-to-shell fit: Staff lives inside the shared app shell through `usePageHeader('Staff', ...)` and `usePageFooter(null, 'status', false)`. The page must not invent a sidebar, top bar, footer, FAB, notification dropdown, or modal layer. Desktop global FAB is suppressed for `/doctors`; mobile uses the route-owned bottom action `Add staff` while the avatar/account menu and bottom island remain shell-owned.
- Read owner: the active page read is `useDoctorsQuery(queryFilter)`, which wraps `doctorsService.getDoctors()`. `PageDataContext` returns no startup domains for `/doctors`, so the shell does not duplicate the Staff collection behind the route-owned query.
- Command owner: Staff create/edit stays inside `DoctorModal` plus `doctorsService.createDoctor()` and `doctorsService.updateDoctor()`. Facility lookup is a scoped read through `getHospitals({ quiet: true, limit: 500 })`. Provider profile linking, invitation, image upload/storage, schedule readiness, assignment, and destructive remove remain excluded until receiver, role authority, and app consequence are proved.
- Responsive owner: desktop keeps status filters, search/filter sheet, grid/list/table modes, pagination, sorting, and detail/edit modal handoff. Mobile uses `MobileDoctors` for compact status filters, search, pull-to-refresh, load more, row reveal, Details, and role-gated Edit. Mobile and desktop share the same product logic and backend owner, but compose differently.
- Today/Requests fit: Staff follows the same single-focus philosophy: one clear add action, local semantic colors for non-danger states, progressive reveal instead of always-open controls, structural skeletons, simple English UI copy, no fake trends, no decorative outer container, and no red/destructive language without proof.
- Current recheck proof: `cmd /c npm test -- --watchAll=false DoctorsPage.contract.test.js PageDataContext.contract.test.js TodayHome.contract.test.js mobileNavigation.contract.test.js VerificationQueue.contract.test.js EmergencyRequestsPage.contract.test.js` passed 6 suites / 53 tests. `cmd /c npm run check:ui-hardgate` passed for 25 files.

Old Git-backed preservation ledger:

| Old Staff behavior from HEAD | Active decision | Current evidence |
|---|---|---|
| `/doctors` route, nav entry, `Medical Staff` header, and SEO title. | Preserve the route and access contract; simplify visible copy to `Staff`. | `routes.jsx`, `navigation.js`, `mobileNavigation.js`, `DoctorsPage.jsx`, and `SEOHead` all use `Staff` while the path remains `/doctors`. |
| Page read used `getDoctors()` plus `PageDataContext` staff data. | Preserve the service read but make the route own it. | `DoctorsPage` calls `useDoctorsQuery(queryFilter)`; `getPageDataStartupDomainsForRole('admin', '/doctors')` and org-admin return `[]`. |
| Admin/org-admin loaded broad staff rows for local filtering and pagination. | Preserve the workflow, but move filtering, exact counts, sort, date range, and pagination to the route-owned service query. | `DoctorsPage.jsx` passes page/mobile limits, offset, search, status/KPI intersection, specialization, date range, and sort into `useDoctorsQuery()`; `doctorsService.getDoctors()` owns exact count and status stats. The page no longer uses `limit: 1000`, `filteredDoctors`, or local row slicing. |
| KPI cards for Total Staff, Available, On Call, Busy, and Off Duty. | Preserve as a Staff status strip and mobile status filters, not dashboard noise. | `StaffFilterStrip` and `mobileStaffFilters` render exact `doctorsService` stats for the current route filter scope while status-card taps feed `kpiFilter` back into the service query. |
| Search, status, specialization, added-date filters, and `FilterSheet`. | Preserve; keep advanced controls progressively disclosed and service-owned. | `filterSchema` includes search, status, specialization, and date; `doctorsService.applyDoctorFilters()` owns the corresponding query filters, and the header keeps one icon trigger with active indicator. |
| Grid, list, table, sorting, pagination, and view toggle. | Preserve desktop modes; remove unproved selection/bulk affordances. | `StaffMemberCard`, `DoctorListView`, `DoctorTableView`, `ViewToggle`, `PaginationControls`, and table sort remain active; sort is submitted to `doctorsService`; `selectedIds` and bulk delete are absent. |
| Mobile doctor directory with pull-to-refresh, load more, row expansion, edit/delete, trend, `LIVE`, and velocity language. | Preserve refresh, load more, row reveal, details, and role-gated edit; remove fake trend/live/delete language. | `MobileDoctors` has `PullToRefresh`, `useLoadMoreControl`, `MobileStaffRow`, `Details`, and `Edit`; stale trend/live/destructive strings are contract-blocked. |
| `DoctorModal` create/view/edit. | Preserve as the canonical Staff modal inside shared `ModalShell`, but narrow it to directory CRUD. | `DoctorModal` uses `ModalShell`, view/create/edit modes, pending save feedback, and toast success/error. |
| Direct Supabase hospital reads, provider profile linking, invitation, and image upload. | Replace hospital read with service lookup; exclude profile linking, invite, and upload. | `DoctorModal` imports `getHospitals`; it does not import direct Supabase, `getProfilesByRole`, `inviteUser`, or `uploadImage`. |
| Single delete, bulk delete, row selection, and confirmation modal. | Exclude from active UI until receiver and consequence proof exist. | Active Staff page/list/table/mobile sources do not contain `deleteDoctor`, `selectedIds`, bulk actions, remove labels, or confirmation modal wiring. |
| Default/fallback rating and experience values. | Do not fabricate rating or experience. | `DoctorModal` normalizes blank experience to null; `doctorsService` uses `rating: input.rating ?? null`, not a fake `4.5`. |
| Analytics modal event and old dashboard-style metrics. | Keep derived status evidence only; do not promote Staff analytics as a canonical pattern. | `openAnalyticsModal` still opens an `AnalyticsModal` with derived stats, but stale mobile velocity/growth copy is removed and Staff is not a global analytics source. |
| Loading and empty states. | Preserve as structural, compact states with next action. | `StaffSkeletonGrid`, `Loading staff`, `One moment`, and `StaffEmptyState` are active; `Add staff` appears only for managing roles. |

## Page 5: Payments

Admission recheck, 2026-06-25:

- Decision: Payments is re-admitted for guarded continuation under the Today/Requests canon. It may proceed after Staff, but only as a money-sensitive page with stricter receiver and fixture proof than ordinary directory pages.
- Route and RBAC: canonical path remains `/wallet`; user-facing label is `Payments`; route protection is `minRole: 'org_admin'`, `resource: 'wallet'`, and `title: 'Payments'`. Admin and org-admin can access; provider, sponsor, and viewer do not get the route.
- Page-to-shell fit: Payments lives inside the shared shell through `usePageHeader('Payments', ...)`, `usePageFooter(null, 'status', false)`, and `usePageShell({ bleed: true, hideFab: true })`. The page may use its own full-bleed signal field, handled activity sheet, and right action rail, but must not create a separate sidebar, top navbar, footer, modal stack, or global FAB behavior.
- Read owner: the active page read is `getWalletPageData({ profile, isAdmin, isOrgAdmin, limit: 50 })` in `walletService`. `PageDataContext` uses `getWalletContextData()` for non-route shell context and returns no startup domains for `/wallet`, so the route owns the page collection and PageData does not duplicate it. The right panel must consume the page-published Payments route context while `/wallet` is active.
- Rendered data: Payments may render wallet balance, projected revenue, saved cards, wallet ledger rows, patient payment rows, patient receipt details, and exportable transactions from `walletService` projections. It must not represent partial, denied, failed, or fixture-limited money data as complete truth.
- Command owner: Add funds, Withdraw, card setup, card list, card delete, and payout-method service calls stay named in `walletService` through Edge Function receivers. The active UI may show safe disabled/unavailable states and guarded modal entry points. Saved-card delete stays service inventory only; actual money-moving submit, card delete, payout setup, cash payment approval, and repair/backfill mutation proof remains excluded until a safe fixture, receiver response, role authority, and app consequence are proved.
- Responsive owner: desktop uses the Today/Requests stage rhythm: atlas-backed signal field, clickable metric choices, handled transaction/payment sheet, and one right action rail. Mobile uses `MobileWallet` for signal first, compact choices, handled bottom sheet, refresh, tab switch, row details, and route-owned actions.
- Today/Requests fit: Payments follows the same calm, single-focus philosophy with simple English copy, one action cluster, structural loading rows, progressive reveal, local non-danger semantic colors, and no decorative outer container. Red stays out of non-danger financial status.
- Current recheck proof: `cmd /c npm test -- --watchAll=false WalletManagementPage.contract.test.js DoctorsPage.contract.test.js PageDataContext.contract.test.js TodayHome.contract.test.js mobileNavigation.contract.test.js VerificationQueue.contract.test.js EmergencyRequestsPage.contract.test.js` passed 7 suites / 62 tests. `cmd /c npm run check:ui-hardgate` passed for 25 files.

Old Git-backed preservation ledger:

| Old Payments behavior from HEAD | Active decision | Current evidence |
|---|---|---|
| `/wallet` route, finance nav, `Wallet & Billing` title, and footer status. | Preserve route/access; simplify visible copy to `Payments` and remove the status footer. | `routes.jsx`, `navigation.js`, `WalletManagementPage`, `MobileWallet`, panels, and modals use `Payments`; `usePageFooter(null, 'status', false)` is active. |
| Page-level Supabase reads for `ivisit_main_wallet`, `organization_wallets`, `wallet_ledger`, `payments`, and profile enrichment. | Preserve the data surface but move reads behind `walletService`. | `WalletManagementPage` calls `getWalletPageData()` and does not call direct Supabase table reads; `getWalletPayments()` uses `profiles!payments_user_id_fkey`. |
| PageData also loaded wallet context. | Preserve non-route context needs without duplicating `/wallet` page startup or right-panel truth. | `PageDataContext` imports `getWalletContextData()`; `getPageDataStartupDomainsForRole('admin', '/wallet')` and org-admin return `[]`; `ContextPanel` consumes `walletRouteContext` for the active Payments right panel. |
| Ledger and patient payment tabs/table. | Preserve as the handled activity sheet, with transaction and patient payment tabs. | `PaymentsSheet` renders `Transaction History` and `Patient Payments`; rows use `PaymentRow` and patient payments open `PaymentReceiptDialog`. |
| CSV ledger export. | Preserve as transaction export, but do not make export imply full financial completeness. | `handleExport()` exports visible ledger rows as `ivisit_transactions_<date>.csv` and shows `Transactions exported.` |
| Auto-run org-admin ledger backfill on page mount. | Exclude from active page load. Repair remains service-only. | `WalletManagementPage` does not import or call `backfillMissingFeeLedger`; the service function remains available for explicit repair proof only. |
| Add funds, withdraw, and billing/card modals. | Preserve guarded modal entry points through shared global financial modals. | Page events pass the route-owned wallet into `GlobalFinancialModals`; amount parsing, card-required, and over-balance guards are active. |
| Payment method list/delete. | Preserve card list/readiness; remove delete from active UI until fixture, receiver, role, and app-consequence proof exist. | Saved-card rows render as read-only evidence; `WalletManagementPage` no longer imports `deletePaymentMethod()` or shows a remove-card affordance. The receiver remains service inventory only. |
| Payout status/readiness and payout method setup. | Exclude from shipped UI until visible flow and receiver proof exist. | Active Payments UI does not show `Payouts ready`, `Setup needed`, or payout setup copy; `setPayoutMethod()` remains service-level only. |
| Mobile wallet KPIs, `LIVE`, trends, ratios, and dashboard terms. | Preserve mobile actions and tabs; remove stale trend/live language. | `MobileWallet` uses Payments signal copy, choices, tabs, and simple empty states instead of `LIVE` or financial-dashboard language. |
| Payment receipt dialog. | Preserve as read-only evidence. | `PaymentReceiptDialog` renders method, service description, patient details, facility, and receipt amount from the selected payment projection. |
| Cash processing and eligibility helpers in `walletService`. | Keep outside active Payments UI unless a pass proves the cash approval workflow. | `process_cash_payment` and `check_cash_eligibility` are service functions, not active Payments page actions. |

Current intake:

- Route: `/wallet`.
- Route/nav authority: protected for `org_admin` and above. The route title and finance nav label now surface as `Payments`, while the canonical path remains `/wallet`.
- Page owner: `WalletManagementPage.jsx`.
- Responsive owner: `MobileWallet.jsx`.
- Read owner: first slice moves page wallet, ledger, payment method, payment, and page-data wallet context reads into `walletService`.
- Command owners: add funds, withdraw, billing card setup, card-delete inventory, and payout method flows remain Edge Function-backed through `walletService` and `GlobalFinancialModals`. Saved-card delete is not an active UI command. Page-load repair/backfill is not an active UI command.

## Page 6: Live Map

Admission recheck, 2026-06-25:

- Decision: Live Map is admitted for guarded continuation under the Today/Requests canon. The route uses the shared full-bleed/no-FAB shell, removes the desktop card-framed map workspace, moves mobile to map-first composition, and replaces old internal visible copy with simple action wording. Desktop and mobile screenshot proof, mobile 390x844 sheet/control geometry proof, route startup ownership, command feedback classification, fallback-renderer parity, route-exit cleanup, and default hardgate promotion have passed. This admits the page; it does not make map layout, mobile sheet, or map action patterns reusable globally.
- Route and RBAC: canonical path remains `/map`; user-facing label is `Live Map`; route protection is `minRole: 'provider'`, `resource: 'map'`, `title: 'Live Map'`, and `excludedRoles: ['sponsor']`. Provider, org-admin, and admin can access; sponsor and viewer are excluded. `ProtectedRoute` enforces this through accessible navigation before min-role checks.
- Page-to-shell fit: Live Map now declares `usePageHeader("Live Map", ...)`, `usePageFooter(null, "status", false)`, and `usePageShell({ bleed: true, hideFab: true })`. Desktop renders the map as the route canvas instead of inside a `Card`; the shared shell yields the global FAB. This admits the page scope, not a reusable shell exception.
- Read owner: current map reads are route-owned by app-level `MapProvider` plus `MapContext`, which sleeps outside `/map`. Initial reads go through `supabaseMapService.fetchInitialMapData({ quiet: true })`, and nearby hospital lookup uses `supabaseMapService.getNearbyHospitals(userLocation, 100, { quiet: true })`. The page does not own raw table reads.
- Realtime owner: `MapContext` subscribes to emergency, ambulance, and user changes only while `/map` is active, and cleans up channels on route exit. PageData startup does not own map collections.
- Command owner: current map commands are named and now classified for this page, but they are not yet a reusable global pattern. Request send/close actions use `emergencyResponseService.dispatchEmergency()` and `completeEmergency()`; responder location uses `updateResponderLocation()`; driver status uses `driverManagementService.updateTripStatus()`; center map and layer toggles are local view commands; refresh calls route-owned `MapContext.refresh()`. Mobile request commands are gated to admin/org-admin like desktop. Send, close, Share, On way, Arrived, Done, refresh, center, and layer toggles now expose visible pressed/pending/confirmation/unavailable feedback; close uses inline confirmation instead of native `confirm()`.
- Responsive owner: `MobileMap` now renders the map as the first layer with a compact top overlay and one selected-marker bottom sheet. This repairs the KPI-strip-first problem. The 390x844 mobile proof confirmed no horizontal overflow, no mobile hamburger, top overlay presence, bottom island presence, and selected-marker controls clearing the sheet and close button.
- Today/Requests fit: Live Map should borrow the canonical ideas, not the Requests markup: one persistent canvas, one active sheet/action, route-owned truth, progressive marker detail, immediate feedback, compact structural loading, local semantic colors, and no fake dispatch/tracking confidence.
- Current recheck proof: `cmd /c npm test -- --watchAll=false GodModeMap.contract.test.js PageDataContext.contract.test.js mobileNavigation.contract.test.js` passed 3 suites / 29 tests after the fallback, route-exit, and shell cleanup repair. `cmd /c npm run check:ui-hardgate` passed for the default 30-file active surface set, including `GodModeMap.jsx`, `MobileMap.jsx`, `MarkerDetailPanel.jsx`, `MapLayerControls.jsx`, and `MapFallback.jsx`. `cmd /c npm run build` passed with bundle `main.eb5ecc87.js`.

## Page 7: Visits

Admission audit start, 2026-06-25:

- Decision: Visits is admitted for guarded continuation under the Today/Requests canon after the 2026-07-02 responsive and deep-link proof. This admits the route as Page 7 only; it does not make Visits a new global layout source, and it does not admit destructive delete, bulk delete, terminal lifecycle outcomes, or clinical/history mutation authority.
- Route and RBAC: canonical path remains `/visits`; user-facing label is `Visits`; route protection is `minRole: 'provider'`, `resource: 'visits'`, `title: 'Visits'`, and `excludedRoles: ['sponsor']`. Provider, org-admin, and admin can access; sponsor and viewer are excluded. Provider mobile bottom navigation remains `Today`, `Requests`, and `Visits`.
- Page-to-shell fit: the first visual implementation pass moved Visits onto the shared full-bleed/no-FAB shell and hid the footer. The content is still the old KPI/grid/list/table workspace, not the final Requests-style stage/sheet/detail rhythm. Any further conversion must preserve or explicitly convert old grid/list/table behavior.
- Read owner: `getVisitsPageData()` in `visitsService` now owns the route list, exact count, route stats, RBAC filter, pagination range, sorting, status/type/date filters, and enrichment reads from `profiles`, `emergency_requests`, `doctors`, and `hospitals`. `VisitsPage` consumes that projection and still owns UI state, modals, and route-local realtime refresh. `/visits` remains route-owned in `pageDataAccess.js`, so `PageDataContext` does not duplicate the `visits` startup domain on the route.
- Realtime owner: current Visits owns a route-local `supabase.channel('visits')` subscription and refreshes the page list on changes. Cleanup now marks the route subscription inactive, removes the Supabase channel, increments the fetch generation, and ignores stale async list results after unmount or superseded refresh. Service-owned realtime semantics are still not promoted as a global pattern.
- Command owner: current create and update paths call `createVisit()` and `updateVisit()` from `visitsService`. Generic create/update writes are now limited to scheduling metadata: patient, facility, date/time, type, scheduling status, notes, cost label, preparation, room, duration, and insurance flag. Terminal outcomes (`completed`, `cancelled`, `no-show`) stay out of the generic form and remain service-command territory. `deleteVisit()` still exists in the service for future receiver/RLS proof, but the active Visits UI no longer imports it or exposes single/bulk delete. Because visits are clinical/history-adjacent evidence, destructive delete remains excluded until backend authority and app consequence are proved.
- Responsive owner: mobile uses `MobileVisits` with `MobilePageShell`, `MobileVisitsAtlasLayer`, service-backed state choices, pull-to-refresh, load-more observer, handled-sheet search, filter trigger, analytics trigger, row reveal, detail action, and role-gated edit. Selection and delete are opt-in props that the active Visits page leaves disabled. The old KPI dashboard stack has been converted into the Requests-style mobile signal/state/sheet rhythm, but this still does not admit the page.
- Today/Requests fit: Visits should borrow Requests' multi-data principles: one clear signal, state choices backed by the route read, a handled activity sheet, focused detail, simple English copy, immediate feedback, and route-owned action. It should not copy Requests markup or invent a second shell.
- Current recheck proof: `cmd /c npm test -- --watchAll=false VisitsPage.contract.test.js PageDataContext.contract.test.js mobileNavigation.contract.test.js EmergencyRequestsPage.contract.test.js` passed 4 suites / 28 tests after the Visits service-owner extraction. The broader admitted-page plus Visits suite passed 9 suites / 78 tests after the extraction. `cmd /c npm run build` passed with bundle `main.d6688c08.js`, including the existing 30-file visual hardgate. Visits is intentionally not in the visual hardgate yet.

Remaining blocker: a backend-owned resolved visit-status projection (server-side status resolution + exact scoped counts) is still required before this route scales past the client resolver limit.

Visits canonical conversion contract:

- Page-to-shell target: convert Visits from normal content into a full-bleed stage using the shared app shell, hidden global FAB, hidden footer, shared module rail, shared top header controls, route-owned mobile action, and no private sidebar, header, footer, notification, or modal chrome.
- Source owner target: keep `visitStatus.js` plus `getVisitsPageData()` as the single Visits source room for raw visit status, linked emergency lifecycle, list rows, exact scoped count, stats, filters, KPI state, sorting, pagination, and enrichment. `PageDataContext` must stay quiet on `/visits`; away from `/visits`, it must use the same source room for summary data. Realtime refresh remains route-owned until a service owner is explicitly promoted.
- First glance target: one signal field for the current visit work, backed by service totals and degraded-state copy. The first visible command is `New visit` for allowed roles only; edit, analytics, emergency context, and filters reveal after selection or explicit tap.
- State choice target: keep `all`, `scheduled`, `in_progress`, `completed`, and `cancelled` as route-query state choices. Local-only mobile or card filtering cannot claim complete truth.
- Activity sheet target: convert old grid/list/table scanning into one handled sheet. Old grid, list, and table views may become sheet density variants only if they read the same projection; otherwise they stay legacy inactive until separately hardgated.
- Focus detail target: add one focused detail rail or sheet for the selected visit. The primary action is `View` or `Edit` only when role and receiver allow it. Terminal status changes, delete, bulk delete, and clinical/lifecycle outcomes remain unavailable unless receiver, RLS, payload, and app consequence are proved.
- Mobile target: keep avatar/account and bottom island as shell-owned navigation. Mobile should render signal, state choices, compact search/filter, handled visit sheet, row reveal, load more, and route-owned `New visit`; it must not restore hamburger navigation, fake trend charts, `LIVE` badges, local-only search/KPI truth, or destructive selection.
- Modal and drawer target: `VisitModal`, `FilterSheet`, `AnalyticsModal`, and `EmergencyDetailsModal` remain allowed secondary surfaces, but modal chrome must use canonical shell patterns. Delete confirmation stays excluded with the destructive action.
- Visual hardgate target: `VisitsPage.jsx`, `MobileVisits.jsx`, `VisitListView.jsx`, `VisitTableView.jsx`, and `VisitModal.jsx` are now in the active UI hardgate because visual implementation has started. Admission still requires focused contracts, hardgate, production build, encoding checks, desktop/mobile rendered proof, interaction feedback proof, and port cleanup.

Old Git-backed preservation ledger:

HEAD snapshot evidence for this ledger: `git show HEAD:frontend/src/components/pages/VisitsPage.jsx` and `git show HEAD:frontend/src/components/mobile/MobileVisits.jsx` showed old `usePageData` consumption, direct page-owned list/count/enrichment reads, grid/list/table modes, filter and analytics modals, confirmation modal, bulk action bar, single/bulk delete handlers, pagination, route realtime, emergency context, mobile pull-to-refresh/load-more, and mobile fake trend chart/local-filter behavior.

| Old Visits behavior from HEAD | Active decision | Current evidence |
|---|---|---|
| `/visits` route and `Visits` nav entry. | Preserve provider access and sponsor exclusion. | `routes.jsx`, `navigation.js`, `mobileNavigation.js`, and `VisitsPage.contract.test.js`. |
| PageDataContext `visitsData`, `refreshAllData`, old page-level stats consumption, and old right-panel `visitsData` consumption. | Converted to route-owned page data and shared summary truth. `PageDataContext` no longer loads the `visits` startup domain while the user is already on `/visits`; away from the route, PageData uses `getVisitsPageData()` so Today summaries and the Visits route share the same status resolver. The page consumes `getVisitsPageData()` plus `visitPageStats` and publishes a route context for the right panel. | `pageDataAccess.js`, `PageDataContext.jsx`, `VisitsPage.jsx`, `ContextPanel.jsx`, `VisitsPanel.jsx`, `visitStatus.js`, `getVisitsPageData()`, and `VisitsPage.contract.test.js`. |
| Page direct exact-count read and paginated list read from `visits`. | Preserved and moved to a service-owned page query before visual admission. | `getVisitsPageData()` owns count/list reads; `VisitsPage.jsx` no longer performs direct `visits` table reads. |
| Enrichment reads for patient profile, emergency request, doctor, and hospital display. | Preserved and moved to the service boundary. | `visitsService.js` enriches route rows from `profiles`, `emergency_requests`, `doctors`, and `hospitals`. |
| Grid/list/table view modes and `ViewToggle`. | Preserved as density variants inside one route-owned activity sheet while the page moves toward the Requests handled-sheet canon. | `VisitsPage.jsx`, `VisitActivitySheet`, `VisitSheetToolbar`, `VisitListView.jsx`, and `VisitTableView.jsx`. |
| KPI/stat cards, search, and filters. | KPI cards are converted into one signal plus service-backed state choices; search is now visible in the activity sheet and writes to the service-owned filter shape; the existing filter drawer remains the explicit secondary reveal. Status filters and KPI filters run after the status source room resolves raw visit status plus linked emergency lifecycle, so `upcoming` rows cannot disappear from all buckets. | `visitStatus.js` and `getVisitsPageData()` own route stats, search, status/type/date filters, and KPI filters; `VisitSignalPanel`, `VisitStateStrip`, `VisitActivitySheet`, `FilterSheet`, and `MobileVisits` render the projection. |
| URL `?view=` deep link opening a visit. | Preserve. | `VisitsPage.jsx` reads `view` and calls `getVisit()`. |
| Create/view/edit visit modal. | Preserve through `VisitModal`; modal chrome is converted to shared `ModalShell`, and generic create/update writes are narrowed to scheduling metadata. Terminal lifecycle outcomes remain outside the generic form. | `VisitModal.jsx`, `ModalShell.jsx`, `createVisit()`, `updateVisit()`, `VISIT_PAGE_WRITE_COLUMNS`, and `VISIT_PAGE_STATUS_VALUES`. |
| Delete and bulk delete. | Excluded from the active Visits UI until receiver/RLS authority and app consequence are proved. | `deleteVisit()` remains service-level inventory only; `VisitsPage.jsx` no longer imports it, no longer renders `BulkActionBar` or delete confirmation, and passes `canDelete={false}` plus `selectionEnabled={false}` to active list/table/mobile surfaces. |
| Confirmation modal for single delete and bulk delete. | Excluded with the destructive delete workflow until backend authority, role scope, confirmation copy, and app consequence are proved. It is not a reusable modal pattern for active Visits. | `VisitsPage.jsx` renders no `ConfirmationModal`; `VisitModal.jsx` uses `ModalShell`; `VisitsPage.contract.test.js` blocks active delete confirmation wiring. |
| Incident/emergency context modal. | Preserve as contextual evidence, not primary page chrome. | `EmergencyDetailsModal` event path. |
| Analytics modal. | Preserve as secondary reveal from route-owned stats. | `AnalyticsModal` receives `visitPageStats`. |
| Right context panel create/statistics entry points. | Preserve event bridges, but convert the panel into a page-owned route-context consumer with local feedback and hardgate-clean copy. | `VisitsPage.jsx` publishes `visitPanelContext`; `ContextPanel.jsx` requests `requestVisitsRouteContext`; `VisitsPanel.jsx` dispatches `openVisitModal` and `openAnalyticsModal` without reading `PageDataContext` `visitsData`. |
| Loading, empty, and failed read states. | Loading stays structural inside the handled sheet, empty directs users to schedule the first visit, and failed route reads now render an in-sheet retry state instead of pretending the list is complete. | `VisitActivitySheet`, `VisitErrorBanner`, `MobileVisitErrorBanner`, `TableSkeleton`, `MobileListSkeletonRows`, `MobileListEmpty`, and `fetchVisits`. |
| Interaction feedback. | Preserve and strengthen immediate feedback: route actions show opening state, state choices show selected state, and row reveal shows open/closed state. | `activeActionFeedback`, `data-state="opening"`, `aria-busy`, `VisitStateStrip`, `VisitsDetailRail`, `VisitSheetToolbar`, and `MobileVisitRow`. |
| Mobile pull-to-refresh, infinite load, search, filter, analytics, row reveal, and edit actions. | Preserved and recomposed into the Requests mobile rhythm: one signal, state choices, compact sheet controls, handled visit sheet, row reveal, load more, and route-owned `New visit`. Destructive delete/selection remains excluded until receiver proof. | `MobileVisits.jsx` renders `MobileVisitsAtlasLayer`, `mobileVisitStates`, `mobile-visits-activity-sheet`, `mobile-visits-sheet-search`, `MobileVisitRow`, pull-to-refresh, load more, `Details`, and role-gated `Edit`; active Visits still passes `canDelete={false}` and `selectionEnabled={false}`. |
| Mobile fake trend charts, dashboard stack, and local-only mobile filtering. | Removed or converted because it presented synthetic momentum and local partial results as operational truth. `Today's Appointments`, `Visit Summary`, and `Visit Directory` are converted into the signal/state/sheet pattern. KPI/search intent goes back to the route owner. | `MobileVisits.jsx` has no `MobileKPIStrip`, `MobileFeaturedMetric`, `MobileSecondaryMetricRail`, `MobileSectionHeader`, `growthData`, `chartData`, fake `LIVE` trend badge, `filteredVisits`, `Active Queue`, `Visit Velocity`, `Today's Appointments`, `Visit Summary`, or `Visit Directory`; `VisitsPage.contract.test.js` locks the removal against old HEAD evidence. |
| Global FAB/mobile action for visit creation. | Converted to route-owned mobile action and hidden desktop global FAB behavior. | `DynamicBottomBar.jsx` dispatches `openVisitModal`; `ContextAwareFAB.jsx` suppresses `/visits`; page header owns desktop create. |

Remaining guarded constraints after Page 7 admission:

- Prove a delete receiver/RLS/app consequence before reintroducing single delete or bulk delete; no clinical/history-adjacent destructive action can ship on UI visibility alone.
- Keep old grid/list/table, modal, filter, realtime, pagination, mobile, and action behavior classifications current if future Visits work changes them.
- Keep active Visits files in the default visual hardgate for the rest of the revamp.
- Re-run focused contract proof, UI hardgate, build, encoding checks, desktop/mobile rendered proof, interaction feedback proof, and local process cleanup after any future Visits visual or workflow change.

## Page 8 Ledger: Hospitals

Audit status: started on 2026-07-02. Decision: Hospitals is admitted for guarded continuation under the Today/Requests canon after the modal, shell, shared-reveal, and default hardgate proof. This admits the route as Page 8 only; it does not make Hospitals a new global layout source, and it does not admit create, destructive delete, staff scheduling, storage upload, or reservation lifecycle authority.

Route and role evidence:

- Route path: `/hospitals`.
- Minimum role: `org_admin`.
- Resource: `hospitals`.
- Navigation group: Care.
- User-facing route title: `Hospitals`.
- Label alignment repair, 2026-07-02: the page header and SEO title now use `Hospitals`, matching route and navigation contracts. Singular create feedback remains `Add facility` while create is unavailable because the table can represent broader facility records and the insert receiver is not proved.

Why Hospitals is Page 8:

- Facility truth feeds Requests, Visits, Live Map, capacity display, pricing context, organization scope, and provider operations.
- A hospital page can accidentally become a parallel source of dispatch, bed, ambulance, scheduling, or organization truth, so it needs an audit gate before design work spreads.

HEAD snapshot evidence for this ledger: `git show HEAD:frontend/src/components/pages/HospitalsPage.jsx` and `git show HEAD:frontend/src/components/mobile/MobileHospitals.jsx` showed old `usePageData` consumption, `hospitalsData` stats, `refreshAllData`, route service reads through `getHospitals()` and `getHospital()`, create/update/delete handlers, direct route realtime, grid/list/table view modes, filter and analytics modals, staff scheduling modal, confirmation modal, bulk action bar, single/bulk delete handlers, pagination, `?id` and `?add=true` URL behavior, custom window events, mobile pull-to-refresh/load-more, mobile search/filter, row reveal, selection, and mobile fake trend/live chart behavior.

### Hospitals Admission Decision

Current decision: preserve the old behavior inventory, keep high-risk actions unavailable until receiver proof, and admit Hospitals only as a guarded route under the Today/Requests canon.

- Route data ownership repair, 2026-07-02: `getHospitalsPageData()` now owns the Hospitals page list, exact count, filters, search, pagination, display-id enrichment through `getHospitals()`, exact status counts, and page-scope visible bed/fleet summaries. `HospitalsPage.jsx` consumes `hospitalPageStats` from that route projection instead of `PageDataContext`, and `pageDataAccess.js` now keeps `PageDataContext` quiet on `/hospitals`.
- Destructive-action exclusion repair, 2026-07-02: active Hospitals UI no longer imports `deleteHospital`, no longer renders delete confirmation or bulk action bars, and passes `canDelete={false}` plus `selectionEnabled={false}` into desktop and mobile surfaces. `deleteHospital()` remains service inventory only. Reintroduction still requires RLS expectation, role authority, payload/identity proof, confirmation copy, facility relationship consequence, app consequence, and safe degraded states.
- Realtime cleanup repair, 2026-07-02: `HospitalsPage.jsx` keeps the route-owned `hospitals_page_changes` channel, but `fetchHospitals()` now uses `isMountedRef` and `fetchRequestRef` guards before mutating rows, count, stats, modal state, loading, or error. The realtime callback checks `active && isMountedRef.current`, and cleanup flips `active = false` before `supabase.removeChannel(channel)`.
- Mobile truth repair, 2026-07-02: `MobileHospitals.jsx` no longer renders `MobileFeaturedMetric`, synthetic `growthData`, synthetic `chartData`, fake `LIVE` trend fallback, or local `filteredHospitals`. Mobile status choices now write to `filters.status`, which is consumed by route-owned `getHospitalsPageData()`, and secondary mobile metrics render exact current counts plus `Visible Beds` and `Visible Fleet` page-scope labels only.
- Action authority repair, 2026-07-02: `updateHospital()` remains active because the maintained `update_hospital_by_admin` RPC checks platform admin or target-organization authority. The route no longer imports `createHospital`, no longer opens create mode for `?add=true` or `openHospitalModal`, and shows `Add facility is unavailable` feedback instead. The shared FAB now treats `/hospitals` as a route-owned action surface, and shared hospital quick actions dispatch to the page-owned unavailable handler. Staff scheduling is excluded from active Hospitals; storage upload is removed from `HospitalModal`; active bed reservation commands are removed and the modal renders `Manage in Requests` as read-only guidance.
- Label alignment repair, 2026-07-02: `HospitalsPage.jsx` now calls `usePageHeader("Hospitals")` and renders `<SEOHead title="Hospitals" />` on desktop and mobile. `HospitalsPage.contract.test.js` blocks `Medical Facilities` from returning to the active page source.
- First-glance signal repair, 2026-07-02: the old desktop KPI opener no longer renders `Network Size`, `At Capacity`, `Low Capacity`, `FILTERED`, `VIEW ALL`, or `Busy / strained`. `HospitalsPage.jsx` now uses `getHospitalSignal()`, `HospitalSignalPanel`, and `HospitalStateStrip` to render one signal field, service-backed `all`, `available`, `full`, and `busy` choices, visible page-scope bed/fleet chips, selected `aria-pressed` state, and motion feedback while preserving the same `kpiFilter` values consumed by `getHospitalsPageData()`.
- Handled facility sheet repair, 2026-07-02: desktop grid/list/table rendering now lives inside `HospitalActivitySheet`, which owns inline service-backed search, filter reveal, exact count/page copy, structural loading, empty state, failed-read retry, and pagination. The shell footer no longer carries page-specific facility count truth.
- Visible degraded-state repair, 2026-07-02: `HospitalsPage.jsx` now stores `hospitalPageError`, clears it on a fresh route read, and renders `HospitalErrorBanner` with a `Retry` action wired to `fetchHospitals()` instead of relying on toast-only failure feedback.
- Focused detail rail repair, 2026-07-02: desktop now uses a Requests-style right rail through `HospitalDetailRail`. The rail follows the focused facility selected from the sheet, displays read-only facility/capacity evidence, keeps reservation changes directed to Requests, and opens the existing view/edit modal paths with immediate feedback instead of creating a second write authority.
- Deep-link modal crash repair, 2026-07-02: rendered proof found `ReferenceError: AnimatePresence is not defined` when `?id=` opened `HospitalModal`. `HospitalModal.jsx` now imports `AnimatePresence` and `motion` from `framer-motion`, restoring the old deep-link view-modal behavior without changing modal authority.
- Desktop grid chrome repair, 2026-07-02: the active desktop Hospitals page no longer imports the shared `Card` wrapper for grid density cards and no longer uses the old decorative grid divider. Grid items are now open selectable facility surfaces that keep the same focus, keyboard, `Details`, and role-gated `Edit` behavior while matching the Requests hardgate direction.
- Mobile hardgate cleanup repair, 2026-07-02: `MobileHospitals.jsx` no longer uses `border-0`, focus ring, outline, or custom cubic-bezier utility tokens on the active mobile search, filter, analytics, expanded-evidence, badge, or row action controls. Pull-to-refresh, load more, route-owned search/status filters, analytics reveal, row reveal, `Details`, role-gated `Edit`, and excluded schedule/delete/selection behavior remain preserved.
- List view hardgate cleanup repair, 2026-07-02: `HospitalListView.jsx` no longer imports the shared `Card` wrapper, no longer uses status badge border tones, and replaces the old rating divider with a soft evidence surface. The list density keeps image fallback, name/status/address/capacity/wait-time/update/rating rendering, hover-revealed `Details`/`Edit`, inactive `Schedule`, and inactive destructive delete gates. Null, empty, or malformed wait-time values now render as `Unknown` instead of `nullm`. Rendered proof also exposed that clicking the selected density toggle could clear all grid/list/table modes; `useViewMode()` now rejects empty or unknown mode values so one density stays active.
- Table view hardgate cleanup repair, 2026-07-02: `HospitalTableView.jsx` no longer imports the shared `Card` wrapper or shared table row/header/body primitives, no longer uses border/ring/outline/divider tokens, replaces structural row separation with inset shadow, and normalizes empty/null wait-time display to `Unknown`. The table density keeps sorting, exact route rows, image fallback, selection/delete inactive gates, mobile row view click, and dropdown `View Details`/`Edit Hospital` actions, with schedule/delete still gated behind proved handlers.
- Hospital modal hardgate cleanup repair, 2026-07-02: `HospitalModal.jsx` keeps shared `ModalShell`, view/edit/create mode inventory, `AnimatePresence` deep-link support, image preview, Google autofill inventory for inactive create, active reservation evidence, and `Manage in Requests` read-only guidance. Its form controls now share a local soft field class and no longer contain border/ring/outline/divider/hairline tokens.
- Shared filter and analytics reveal hardgate cleanup repair, 2026-07-02: shared `FilterSheet.jsx` and `AnalyticsModal.jsx` no longer contain border/ring/outline/divider/hairline tokens. Filter inputs use the same soft focus-shadow pattern, analytics separators are non-hairline dots, and the analytics action copy now uses simple English labels.
- Hospitals shell repair, 2026-07-02: `HospitalsPage.jsx` now calls `usePageShell({ bleed: true, hideFab: true })`, keeps the shared shell header/footer ownership, and uses the page-owned `Add facility` unavailable action instead of a global FAB.
- The first touched Hospitals source pass removed visible non-ASCII/mojibake from `HospitalsPage.jsx`; later touched active Hospitals and shared reveal files passed encoding review.

### Hospitals Required Repair Before Admission

- Keep `getHospitalsPageData()` as the named route-owned hospital page data query for list, count, filters, stats, search, pagination, and display IDs.
- Do not use a capped full-row hospital fetch as a network total, status count, or KPI source; use exact count queries or label the number as page-scope.
- Keep the canonical route/page label as `Hospitals` across route config, navigation, `SmartHeader`, SEO title, mobile shell, and rendered browser proof.
- Keep route-owned realtime cleanup guards current after future Hospitals changes.
- Keep mobile stats/current-state only until a real historical trend source is named.
- Keep create unavailable until an insert/RPC receiver and RLS expectation are proved.
- Keep staff scheduling unavailable until doctor/ambulance status receivers and app consequences are proved.
- Keep reservation lifecycle actions in Requests; do not restore cancel/arrived/discharge buttons to `HospitalModal` without receiver, role, confirmation, and app-consequence proof.
- Keep the handled facility sheet as the owner of desktop search, pagination, loading, empty, and failed-read states.
- Keep the focused detail rail as read-only evidence; do not add write controls outside proved modal/RPC paths.
- Keep `HospitalModal` motion imports locked because `?id=` deep links crash without `AnimatePresence` and `motion`.
- Keep the active desktop Hospitals page source hardgate-clean; do not reintroduce the shared `Card` wrapper, decorative grid divider, or border/ring/hairline tokens.
- Keep the active mobile Hospitals source hardgate-clean; do not reintroduce border/ring/outline/divider/hairline tokens or stale custom easing utilities into mobile search, filter, analytics, expanded evidence, badge, or action controls.
- Keep the active Hospitals list density source hardgate-clean; do not reintroduce the shared `Card` wrapper, status border tones, decorative rating divider, or border/ring/outline/divider/hairline tokens.
- Keep the active Hospitals table density source hardgate-clean; do not reintroduce shared `Card` or table row primitives, status border tones, structural row borders, or border/ring/outline/divider/hairline tokens.
- Keep the active Hospitals modal source hardgate-clean; do not reintroduce border/ring/outline/divider/hairline tokens into `HospitalModal.jsx`.
- Keep shared FilterSheet and AnalyticsModal hardgate-clean because Hospitals, Visits, Staff, and Approvals use them as secondary reveals.
- Keep shared MobileMetricList hardgate-clean because Hospitals mobile row reveal, Staff/Users, Ambulances, and other mobile directory rows inherit its interaction chrome.
- Keep Hospitals in the default UI hardgate with its desktop page, mobile page, shared mobile metric row, list density, table density, entity modal, shared filter sheet, and shared analytics modal.
- Keep `PageDataContext` quiet on `/hospitals`, matching the Requests, Approvals, Staff, Payments, Map, and Visits pattern.
- Classify every create/update/delete/schedule action by role, backend receiver, payload, RLS expectation, unavailable state, and app consequence.
- Keep single delete, bulk delete, and destructive mobile selection excluded until facility deletion is proved safe across related visits, requests, ambulances, doctors, organization scope, and app-visible facility search.
- Keep the converted first glance on the Requests canon: one signal, service-backed state choices, visible page-scope bed/fleet chips, and one primary action.
- Preserve old grid/list/table behavior only as density variants inside the handled sheet.
- Preserve `?id` with browser proof on desktop, tablet, mobile, and deep-link modal states. Keep `?add=true` as unavailable feedback until create proof exists.

## Page 9 Intake Audit: Ambulances

Audit status: started on 2026-07-02. Decision: Ambulances is admitted for guarded continuation under the Today/Requests canon after the 2026-07-03 context-panel, tablet, mobile polish, interaction, hardgate, build, encoding, and cleanup proof. This ledger represents old Git-backed behavior and current contract risks. This does not make fleet CRUD, destructive delete, driver assignment, image upload, status/location mutation, or active-trip commands globally safe.

Route and role evidence:

- Route path: `/ambulances`.
- Minimum role: `org_admin`.
- Resource: `ambulances`.
- Navigation group: Care.
- User-facing route title: `Ambulances`.
- Visible roles: admin and org admin.
- Blocked roles: provider, sponsor, and viewer.

Why Ambulances is Page 9:

- It is the next Care route after Hospitals and shares facility, staff, Requests, Live Map, and responder truth.
- Fleet rows affect app-facing dispatch, tracking, driver assignment, active trip status, and facility readiness.
- The maintained Provider Operations contract already names fleet status vocabulary, organization/hospital identity, station labels, rating, busy/on-route metrics, direct status/location writers, modal active-trip scope, and capped pagination as Pass 5 risks.

HEAD snapshot evidence for this ledger: `git show HEAD:frontend/src/components/pages/AmbulancesPage.jsx`, `git show HEAD:frontend/src/components/mobile/MobileAmbulances.jsx`, `git show HEAD:frontend/src/components/modals/AmbulanceModal.jsx`, and `git show HEAD:frontend/src/services/ambulancesService.js` showed old `usePageData` consumption, `ambulancesData` stats, `refreshAllData`, direct route Supabase list/count reads, `applyAuthFilter`, `limit(1000)` client paging, grid/list/table view modes, `ViewToggle`, filter sheet, analytics modal, create/view/edit modal, confirmation modal, single delete, bulk delete, selected IDs, pagination footer, `?add=true` behavior, context-panel events, mobile pull-to-refresh, infinite load, mobile search/filter, KPI choices, local mobile filtering, fake trend/chart data, row reveal, mobile view/edit/delete actions, private modal chrome, modal direct Supabase reads, image upload, provider profile linking, broad driver occupancy reads, hospital-wide trip controls, default `rating: 4.5`, and the old org-id-as-hospital-id fallback. Since intake, the worktree has added route-owned fleet page data, a zero-domain startup override for `/ambulances`, page consumption of that service, mobile truth cleanup, inactive destructive delete/selection capabilities, and canonical modal shell/scope cleanup while preserving the old modal, view-mode, filter, pagination, analytics, and CRUD inventory for later authority review.

### Ambulances Current Source Inventory

- `App.js`, `routes.jsx`, and `navigation.js` keep `/ambulances` protected at org-admin scope with the `Ambulances` Care nav label.
- `AmbulancesPage.jsx` owns the current page, uses `usePageHeader()`, `usePageFooter(null, 'status', false)`, and `usePageShell({ bleed: true, hideFab: true })`, and now consumes `getAmbulancesPageData()` for rows, exact count, stats, filters, pagination, sorting, status alias mapping, and station enrichment.
- `pageDataAccess.js` now makes `/ambulances` route-owned with a zero-domain startup override, so `PageDataContext` no longer duplicates generic fleet truth while this route is open.
- The old direct Supabase count/list read, capped `limit(1000)` fetch, org-admin `hospital_id = orgId` stats path, and `PageDataContext` stats consumption have been removed from `AmbulancesPage.jsx`.
- `ambulancesService.js` owns generic CRUD plus `assignDriverToAmbulance()`, `updateAmbulanceLocation()`, `updateAmbulanceStatus()`, available drivers, available ambulances, hospital ambulances, and realtime subscription helpers.
- `useAmbulances.js` exposes the same generic list, create, update, delete, location, status, and subscription operations, but the active route does not yet consume it as a named page owner.
- `AmbulanceModal.jsx` now uses shared `ModalShell`, loads station options through `getHospitals({ quiet: true, limit: 500 })`, narrows submit payloads to fields accepted by `createAmbulance()` and `updateAmbulance()`, sets `organization_id` rather than `hospital_id` for org-admin create context, and renders driver link, media, and trip commands as unavailable/read-only notes until those receivers are proved.
- `AmbulanceListView.jsx` and `AmbulanceTableView.jsx` preserve desktop density variants, row actions, table sorting, selection inventory, and dropdown actions. Active density variants no longer fall back to `HQ`, and the active table no longer renders `ambulance.rating` as fleet truth.
- `MobileAmbulances.jsx` preserves mobile pull-to-refresh, load-more observer, search, filter, analytics trigger, KPI choices, row reveal, details, and edit actions. It no longer renders synthetic featured metric charts, `LIVE` trend copy, row-derived rating, local-only filtered rows, or mobile delete/selection actions.
- `AmbulancesPanel.jsx` now renders the typed route summary published by `AmbulancesPage.jsx`, with loading, failed, empty, and ready states from the page-owned projection. It no longer consumes broad `PageDataContext` fleet rows or calls stale readiness copy live.

### Ambulances Canonical Conversion Target

- Page-to-shell visual-start: Ambulances now uses the shared route-owned stage through `usePageShell({ bleed: true, hideFab: true })`, shared header controls, hidden page footer truth, shared rail, shared mobile avatar and bottom island, and no private sidebar, notification, modal chrome, or global FAB.
- Source owner target: keep `getAmbulancesPageData()` as the named fleet page query owner. It owns exact counts, server-side pagination, filters, search, sort, station join/display, role scope, and status normalization. Degraded states and realtime ownership still need visual-pass proof.
- First glance visual-start: the five old desktop stat cards are converted into one fleet signal plus service-backed state choices. Avoid red for non-danger busy or unavailable states, and do not show rating, busy, or trend copy unless the source proves those meanings.
- Activity visual-start: old grid/list/table behavior is preserved as density variants inside one handled fleet sheet. Density changes must not change action authority.
- Focus visual-start: the page has one read-first focused detail rail for the selected unit. Primary actions are `Details` and `Edit` only when role and receiver proof allow them.
- Mobile target: keep pull-to-refresh, compact state choices, search/filter, handled fleet sheet, row reveal, details, edit, and load more. Keep fake trend/chart data, `LIVE` labels, local-only KPI/search truth, and destructive selection excluded until receiver proof exists.
- Modal source repair: create/view/edit inventory now uses the canonical modal pattern and separates ordinary fleet details from active-trip lifecycle commands. A vehicle modal cannot command hospital-wide trips unless each request is proved linked to that vehicle.
- Context-panel target: preserve Add/Data/Filter entry points through the shell, but use typed route feedback and do not render broad or stale context snapshots as live readiness.

| Grid/list/table view modes and `ViewToggle`. | Preserve as density variants inside one handled fleet sheet after action authority and hardgate cleanup. | `AmbulancesPage.jsx` renders `AmbulanceActivitySheet` around the grid/list/table variants; `AmbulanceListView.jsx` and `AmbulanceTableView.jsx` are hardgate-clean and destructive affordances remain disabled unless explicitly capability-proved. |

Status values must match the backend enum, and display-only busy/rating claims must not imply backend truth. | `AmbulanceSignalPanel`, `AmbulanceStateStrip`, `MobileAmbulances.jsx`, and `PROVIDER_OPERATIONS_CONTRACT_CHART_2026-05-24.md`. |
| Analytics modal. | Preserve as a secondary reveal from route-owned scoped fleet stats. | `AnalyticsModal` receives `ambulancePageStats` through `displayStats`. |

Full admission still needs rendered modal and interaction proof. | `AmbulanceModal.jsx`, `ambulancesService.js`, and `AmbulancesPage.contract.test.js`. |

Existing media is only acknowledged as unavailable to edit here. | Old HEAD modal called `uploadImage(file, 'ambulances')`; active `AmbulanceModal.jsx` does not import `uploadImage` and renders `Media upload needs storage policy proof.` |

Active `AmbulancesPage.jsx` no longer imports `deleteAmbulance`, renders `ConfirmationModal`, renders `BulkActionBar`, or passes delete/selection props to mobile/list/table. |

| Service/hook realtime subscription helpers. | Preserve as inventory, but do not claim the page has route-owned realtime until a mounted owner and cleanup behavior are proved. | `subscribeToAmbulance()`, `subscribeToAllAmbulances()`, and `useAmbulances.subscribeAll()`. |

Toast-only failures and complete-looking empty states are not enough. | `AmbulanceActivitySheet`, `AmbulanceErrorBanner`, `AmbulanceEmptyState`, `TableSkeleton`, `MobileListSkeletonRows`, and `MobileListEmpty`. |

### Ambulances Required Repair Before Admission

Route data owner repair, 2026-07-02:

- `getAmbulancesPageData()` now owns the route list, exact count, stats, filters, pagination, status alias mapping, sorting, and station enrichment.
- `/ambulances` now has a zero-domain startup override, so `PageDataContext` does not duplicate fleet truth while the route is open.
- `AmbulancesPage.jsx` no longer imports `usePageData`, direct `supabase`, `getCurrentUser`, `applyAuthFilter`, or `withTimeout` for its route read.
- The old `on_route` UI value maps to backend `en_route`, and the old `busy` KPI maps to active fleet statuses rather than a persisted `busy` status.
- Station display is enriched from linked hospital names when `hospital_id` is available.

Mobile truth and destructive action repair, 2026-07-02:

- `MobileAmbulances.jsx` no longer renders synthetic featured metric charts, `LIVE` trend copy, local-only filtered rows, row-derived rating, or mobile delete/selection actions.
- Mobile KPI taps still call `setKpiFilter`, and mobile search still writes `filters.search`, so route-owned `getAmbulancesPageData()` remains the read owner for filtered results.
- `AmbulancesPage.jsx` no longer imports `deleteAmbulance`, renders `BulkActionBar`, opens `ConfirmationModal` for delete, or passes active delete/selection props to mobile, list, or table density views.
- `AmbulanceListView.jsx` and `AmbulanceTableView.jsx` now require explicit `canDelete` capability before rendering destructive controls; the table also requires explicit `selectionEnabled` before rendering row selection.

Visual shell and first-glance repair, 2026-07-02:

- `AmbulancesPage.jsx` now uses `usePageShell({ bleed: true, hideFab: true })` and `usePageFooter(null, 'status', false)`, matching the Requests/Hospitals/Visits full-stage shell contract.
- The five old desktop stat cards became `AmbulanceSignalPanel` and `AmbulanceStateStrip`, with service-backed counts and non-danger tones for normal fleet states.
- `AmbulanceActivitySheet` owns desktop search, filter and analytics triggers, density controls, exact-count copy, structural loading, retry error, empty state, pagination, and the grid/list/table variants.
- `AmbulanceDetailRail` owns focused read-first unit evidence plus `Details` and `Edit` triggers with immediate action feedback.
- `AmbulanceListView.jsx` and `AmbulanceTableView.jsx` no longer fall back to `HQ`; `AmbulanceTableView.jsx` no longer renders `ambulance.rating`.
- The active Ambulances page, mobile, list, and table surfaces are in the default UI hardgate.
- `AmbulanceModal.jsx` was added to the default UI hardgate after modal scope, field persistence, and trip-command ownership were repaired at source level.
- Rendered Browser proof used one local dev server on `127.0.0.1:3000`: desktop `1280x900` at `/ambulances?proof=ambulances-visual-start` rendered `Ambulances | iVisit Console`, showed `Add unit` without all-caps text, displayed the signal panel, handled sheet, sheet search, and detail rail, had no `Fleet Management` title/copy, and had no horizontal overflow. - Mobile Browser proof at `390x844` rendered `Ambulances | iVisit Console`, showed `Fleet Signals`, `Fleet Directory`, and mobile search, kept `Delete`, `LIVE`, and `Fleet Management` absent, and had no horizontal overflow. At this checkpoint the proof was visual-start only; the modal, tablet, context-panel, and interaction gates are closed by the 2026-07-03 proof sections below.

Modal scope and chrome repair, 2026-07-03:

- `AmbulanceModal.jsx` now uses shared `ModalShell` for backdrop, ARIA dialog semantics, scroll containment, and shell chrome suppression.
- The active modal no longer imports direct Supabase, `uploadImage`, `getProfilesByRole`, `getDrivers`, `assignDriverToAmbulance`, or `driverManagementService`.
- The active modal no longer defaults `rating` to `4.5`, no longer writes `orgId` into `hospital_id`, and no longer renders private `fixed inset-0` dialog chrome.
- Station options load through `getHospitals({ quiet: true, limit: 500 })`; org-admin create context writes `organization_id` only.
- Submit payloads are narrowed to service-accepted fleet fields: call sign, type, status, vehicle number, license plate, station, ETA, crew note, current call, base price, and organization id.
- Trip-owned statuses render as read-only guidance, and driver link, media, and trip commands render unavailable notes instead of active controls.
- `AmbulanceModal.jsx` is now in the default UI hardgate with the active Ambulances page, mobile, list, and table sources.

Context-panel truth repair, 2026-07-03:

- `AmbulancesPage.jsx` now publishes a compact route-owned `ambulancesRouteContextUpdated` summary from the same service-backed rows and stats that render the page.
- `ContextPanel.jsx` no longer passes global `PageDataContext` `ambulancesData` into the Ambulances panel. When the right context panel opens on `/ambulances`, it requests the active route summary with `requestAmbulancesRouteContext` and renders only that page-owned projection.
- `AmbulancesPanel.jsx` now receives `ambulanceContext`, renders `Fleet context` / `From this page`, shows loading, failed, empty, and ready states, and keeps recent rows to the page-owned five-row summary. It no longer renders stale `Live Fleet` or `Ready Units` claims from a broad global cache.
- The shell context action for `/ambulances` now says `Add unit` and dispatches `openAmbulanceModal`, so route-owned feedback and modal handling stay in `AmbulancesPage.jsx`; it no longer opens a private global `AmbulanceModal` through `openModal('ambulance')`.
- `ContextAwareFAB.jsx` treats `/ambulances` as a route-owned action page alongside Requests, Visits, Staff, Hospitals, Map, and Payments.
- `AmbulancesPanel.jsx` is now in the default UI hardgate with the active Ambulances page, mobile, list, table, and modal sources.

- Keep the named fleet page read owner current as visual implementation proceeds.
- Keep `/ambulances` route-owned in startup data so the shell does not duplicate fleet truth on the route.
- Keep server-backed paging, exact count, search, filters, and sort; do not restore capped client pagination as complete truth.
- Normalize status vocabulary to the maintained backend enum. Do not use `on_route` where the database uses `en_route`, and do not treat `busy` as a persisted ambulance status unless a backend owner maps it.
- Join or normalize station display from `hospital_id` to hospital name. Do not fall back to `HQ` for a correctly linked unit in the revamped first glance.
- Keep organization IDs and hospital IDs distinct. Do not submit `orgId` as `hospital_id`, and do not let a driver profile organization overwrite a station hospital ID.
- Treat rating, image, last maintenance, and any non-table field as unavailable, read-only, or service-proved before rendering them as saved truth.
- Split ordinary fleet CRUD from active dispatch/tracking commands. Status/location writes for active trips must use request-coupled receivers.
- Keep active-trip controls out of `AmbulanceModal` until they are scoped to the opened ambulance or routed to a proved Requests/Map lifecycle surface.
- Keep single delete, bulk delete, and destructive mobile selection excluded from the revamped page until destructive receiver proof and app consequence are complete.
- Apply one capability projection before grid, list, table, mobile, and modal renderers receive edit/delete/driver/status callbacks.
- Convert context-panel readiness into a typed scoped summary with failed, degraded, empty, and unavailable states. Do not call stale or broad context rows `Live Fleet`.
- Keep fake mobile trend/chart/live claims and local-only mobile filtering out of active mobile Ambulances.
- Keep `AmbulanceModal` on canonical modal chrome; do not restore driver assignment, media upload, or active-trip commands until receiver, role, payload, and app consequence are proved.
- Keep `AmbulanceModal.jsx` in the default UI hardgate with the active Ambulances surfaces.
- Keep `AmbulancesPanel.jsx` in the default UI hardgate with the active Ambulances surfaces.
- Admission proof completed on 2026-07-03: focused contract tests, default and focused UI hardgate, production build, encoding checks, tablet proof, rendered context-panel proof, interaction feedback proof, mobile polish proof, Browser viewport reset, and local port cleanup. Future Ambulances changes must rerun the relevant proof slice before preserving this admission state.
